theorem lowerStep_add (a b : ratRep) (p : Partition)
    (f g : ratRep → ratRep) (l m : Nat → ratRep)
    (hl : LowerStep a b p f l) (hm : LowerStep a b p g m) :

练习 5upperSum_add定理附录解答

上和与下和各自分开

theorem upperSum_add (a b : ratRep) (p : Partition)
    (f g : ratRep → ratRep) (U V : ratRep)
    (hU : UpperSum a b p f U) (hV : UpperSum a b p g V) :
    UpperSum a b p (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f x) (g x))
      (ratAddRep U V) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

从上和里取出上界 u，从第二条里取出 v；两摞逐段相加就是和的上界，矩形和按 stepSum_add 分成两个，两个候选值也相应相加。

提示 2目标形态

UpperSum a b p (fun x => f x + g x) (U + V)。

提示 3关键件

upperSum_witness · upperStep_add · stepSum_add · ratAddRep_wd_gen · ratAddRep_wd_snd_gen。

查看解答 定理 · 29 行

从两个上和里各取出一摞上界。逐段相加就是和的上界；矩形和按 stepSum_add 分成两个，候选值也随之相加。两处候选值都只是等价写法，用一次两边同加的 搬运把它们接到 U + V 上。

theorem upperSum_add (a b : ratRep) (p : Partition)
    (f g : ratRep → ratRep) (U V : ratRep)
    (hU : UpperSum a b p f U) (hV : UpperSum a b p g V) :
    UpperSum a b p (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f x) (g x))
      (ratAddRep U V) :=
  Exists.elim (upperSum_witness a b p f U hU)
    (fun (u : Nat → ratRep)
        (hu : UpperStep a b p f u ∧ ratRel U (stepSum p u)) =>
      Exists.elim (upperSum_witness a b p g V hV)
        (fun (v : Nat → ratRep)
            (hv : UpperStep a b p g v ∧ ratRel V (stepSum p v)) =>
          Exists.intro (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (u i) (v i))
            (And.intro
              (upperStep_add a b p f g u v (And.left hu) (And.left hv))
              (ratRel_trans_gen
                (ratAddRep U V)
                (ratAddRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p v))
                (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (u i) (v i)))
                (ratRel_trans_gen
                  (ratAddRep U V)
                  (ratAddRep (stepSum p u) V)
                  (ratAddRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p v))
                  (ratAddRep_wd_gen U (stepSum p u) V (And.right hu))
                  (ratAddRep_wd_snd_gen (stepSum p u) V (stepSum p v)
                    (And.right hv)))
                (ratRel_symm
                  (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (u i) (v i)))
                  (ratAddRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p v))
                  (stepSum_add p u v))))))

theorem lowerSum_add (a b : ratRep) (p : Partition)

    (hL : LowerSum a b p f L) (hM : LowerSum a b p g M) :

      (ratAddRep L M) :=

提示 1思路

与上和同形，把下界 l、m 逐段相加。

提示 2目标形态

LowerSum a b p (fun x => f x + g x) (L + M)。

提示 3关键件

lowerSum_witness · lowerStep_add · stepSum_add · ratAddRep_wd_gen · ratAddRep_wd_snd_gen。

查看解答 定理 · 29 行

下和同形：两摞下界逐段相加，矩形和分成两个，候选值相加。

theorem lowerSum_add (a b : ratRep) (p : Partition)
    (f g : ratRep → ratRep) (L M : ratRep)
    (hL : LowerSum a b p f L) (hM : LowerSum a b p g M) :
    LowerSum a b p (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f x) (g x))
      (ratAddRep L M) :=
  Exists.elim (lowerSum_witness a b p f L hL)
    (fun (l : Nat → ratRep)
        (hl : LowerStep a b p f l ∧ ratRel L (stepSum p l)) =>
      Exists.elim (lowerSum_witness a b p g M hM)
        (fun (m : Nat → ratRep)
            (hm : LowerStep a b p g m ∧ ratRel M (stepSum p m)) =>
          Exists.intro (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (l i) (m i))
            (And.intro
              (lowerStep_add a b p f g l m (And.left hl) (And.left hm))
              (ratRel_trans_gen
                (ratAddRep L M)
                (ratAddRep (stepSum p l) (stepSum p m))
                (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (l i) (m i)))
                (ratRel_trans_gen
                  (ratAddRep L M)
                  (ratAddRep (stepSum p l) M)
                  (ratAddRep (stepSum p l) (stepSum p m))
                  (ratAddRep_wd_gen L (stepSum p l) M (And.right hl))
                  (ratAddRep_wd_snd_gen (stepSum p l) M (stepSum p m)
                    (And.right hm)))
                (ratRel_symm
                  (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (l i) (m i)))
                  (ratAddRep (stepSum p l) (stepSum p m))
                  (stepSum_add p l m))))))
练习 7stepGap_add定理附录解答

两块面积之差的和

theorem stepGap_add (p : Partition) (l u m v : Nat → ratRep) :
    ratRel
      (ratSubRep (ratAddRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p v))
        (ratAddRep (stepSum p l) (stepSum p m)))
      (ratAddRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l))
        (ratSubRep (stepSum p v) (stepSum p m))) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

(U + V) - (L + M) 与 (U - L) + (V - M) 只差一次重排；两个差相加成一个大差的那条引理反过来用。

提示 2目标形态

ratRel ((U + V) - (L + M)) ((U - L) + (V - M))。

提示 3关键件

ratAdd_sub_sub · ratRel_symm。

查看解答 定理 · 13 行

「两个差相加成一个大差」的反方向就是这一条：和的差拆成两个差的和。

theorem stepGap_add (p : Partition) (l u m v : Nat → ratRep) :
    ratRel
      (ratSubRep (ratAddRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p v))
        (ratAddRep (stepSum p l) (stepSum p m)))
      (ratAddRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l))
        (ratSubRep (stepSum p v) (stepSum p m))) :=
  ratRel_symm
    (ratAddRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l))
      (ratSubRep (stepSum p v) (stepSum p m)))
    (ratSubRep (ratAddRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p v))
      (ratAddRep (stepSum p l) (stepSum p m)))
    (ratAdd_sub_sub (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l) (stepSum p v)
      (stepSum p m))
练习 8stepGap_add_lt定理附录解答
theorem stepGap_add_lt (p : Partition) (l u m v : Nat → ratRep)
    (eps : ratRep)
    (hf : ratLtRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l))
      (ratHalf eps))
    (hg : ratLtRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p v) (stepSum p m))
      (ratHalf eps)) :
    ratLtRep
      (ratSubRep (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (u i) (v i)))
        (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (l i) (m i)))) eps :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

两个差都小于 ε/2，加起来就小于 ε/2 + ε/2；而 ε/2 + ε/2 等于 ε，最后沿等式把右端换成 ε。左边是「和的差」，先换成「两个差的和」，再接上两个已知的严格界。

提示 2目标形态

ratLtRep (stepSum (fun i => u i + v i) - stepSum (fun i => l i + m i)) eps。

提示 3关键件

ratLtRep_add · ratHalf_add_self · ratSubRep_wd · ratAdd_sub_sub · ratLtRep_congr_fst · ratLtRep_congr_snd。

查看解答 定理 · 44 行

两个差相加先小于 ε/2 + ε/2，再沿「半数加半数等于 ε」把右端换成 ε。左边 是「两个差的和」，先把和的差拆成它，才能接上两个已知的严格界。

theorem stepGap_add_lt (p : Partition) (l u m v : Nat → ratRep)
    (eps : ratRep)
    (hf : ratLtRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l))
      (ratHalf eps))
    (hg : ratLtRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p v) (stepSum p m))
      (ratHalf eps)) :
    ratLtRep
      (ratSubRep (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (u i) (v i)))
        (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (l i) (m i)))) eps :=
  ratLtRep_congr_fst
    (ratSubRep (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (u i) (v i)))
      (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (l i) (m i))))
    (ratAddRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l))
      (ratSubRep (stepSum p v) (stepSum p m)))
    eps
    (ratLtRep_congr_snd
      (ratAddRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l))
        (ratSubRep (stepSum p v) (stepSum p m)))
      (ratAddRep (ratHalf eps) (ratHalf eps))
      eps
      (ratLtRep_add (ratSubRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l))
        (ratHalf eps) (ratSubRep (stepSum p v) (stepSum p m))
        (ratHalf eps) hf hg)
      (ratHalf_add_self eps))
    (ratRel_trans_gen
      (ratSubRep (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (u i) (v i)))
        (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (l i) (m i))))
      (ratSubRep (ratAddRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p v))
        (ratAddRep (stepSum p l) (stepSum p m)))
      (ratAddRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l))
        (ratSubRep (stepSum p v) (stepSum p m)))
      (ratSubRep_wd
        (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (u i) (v i)))
        (ratAddRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p v))
        (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (l i) (m i)))
        (ratAddRep (stepSum p l) (stepSum p m))
        (stepSum_add p u v) (stepSum_add p l m))
      (ratRel_symm
        (ratAddRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l))
          (ratSubRep (stepSum p v) (stepSum p m)))
        (ratSubRep (ratAddRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p v))
          (ratAddRep (stepSum p l) (stepSum p m)))
        (ratAdd_sub_sub (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l) (stepSum p v)
          (stepSum p m))))
练习 9integrable_add_of_gap定理附录解答

同一个分割上的可加性

theorem integrable_add_of_gap (a b : ratRep)
    (f g : ratRep → ratRep)
    (h : ∀ (eps : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep eps →
      ∃ (p : Partition), IsPartition a b p ∧
        ∃ (l : Nat → ratRep), ∃ (u : Nat → ratRep),
          ∃ (m : Nat → ratRep), ∃ (v : Nat → ratRep),
            LowerStep a b p f l ∧ UpperStep a b p f u ∧
            LowerStep a b p g m ∧ UpperStep a b p g v ∧
            ratLtRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l))
              (ratHalf eps) ∧
            ratLtRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p v) (stepSum p m))
              (ratHalf eps)) :
    Integrable a b (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f x) (g x)) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

把可积性的判据拆开：给定 ε，取出分割与四条台阶；和的下界取 l + m、上界取 u + v，两条台阶引理给出它们夹住和，差由 stepGap_add_lt 压到 ε 以下。最后把两个「存在」交给判据的另一个方向。

提示 2目标形态

Integrable a b (fun x => f x + g x)。

提示 3关键件

integrable_of_gap · upperStep_add · lowerStep_add · stepGap_add_lt · Exists.elim · Exists.intro。

查看解答 定理 · 92 行

判据要的是「每个正 ε 都有一套台阶」。假设正好给出这一套：分割、两条下界、 两条上界、两个差各自小于 ε/2。四条台阶逐段相加，得到和的下界与上界； 两个差由上一节压成和的一个差，仍然小于 ε。于是判据的另一个方向接上了。

theorem integrable_add_of_gap (a b : ratRep)
    (f g : ratRep → ratRep)
    (h : ∀ (eps : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep eps →
      ∃ (p : Partition), IsPartition a b p ∧
        ∃ (l : Nat → ratRep), ∃ (u : Nat → ratRep),
          ∃ (m : Nat → ratRep), ∃ (v : Nat → ratRep),
            LowerStep a b p f l ∧ UpperStep a b p f u ∧
            LowerStep a b p g m ∧ UpperStep a b p g v ∧
            ratLtRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l))
              (ratHalf eps) ∧
            ratLtRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p v) (stepSum p m))
              (ratHalf eps)) :
    Integrable a b (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f x) (g x)) :=
  integrable_of_gap a b (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f x) (g x))
    (fun (eps : ratRep) (heps : ratLtRep ratZeroRep eps) =>
      Exists.elim (h eps heps)
        (fun (p : Partition)
            (hp : IsPartition a b p ∧
              ∃ (l : Nat → ratRep), ∃ (u : Nat → ratRep),
                ∃ (m : Nat → ratRep), ∃ (v : Nat → ratRep),
                  LowerStep a b p f l ∧ UpperStep a b p f u ∧
                  LowerStep a b p g m ∧ UpperStep a b p g v ∧
                  ratLtRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l))
                    (ratHalf eps) ∧
                  ratLtRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p v) (stepSum p m))
                    (ratHalf eps)) =>
          Exists.elim (And.right hp)
            (fun (l : Nat → ratRep)
                (hl : ∃ (u : Nat → ratRep), ∃ (m : Nat → ratRep),
                  ∃ (v : Nat → ratRep),
                    LowerStep a b p f l ∧ UpperStep a b p f u ∧
                    LowerStep a b p g m ∧ UpperStep a b p g v ∧
                    ratLtRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l))
                      (ratHalf eps) ∧
                    ratLtRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p v) (stepSum p m))
                      (ratHalf eps)) =>
              Exists.elim hl
                (fun (u : Nat → ratRep)
                    (hu : ∃ (m : Nat → ratRep), ∃ (v : Nat → ratRep),
                      LowerStep a b p f l ∧ UpperStep a b p f u ∧
                      LowerStep a b p g m ∧ UpperStep a b p g v ∧
                      ratLtRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l))
                        (ratHalf eps) ∧
                      ratLtRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p v) (stepSum p m))
                        (ratHalf eps)) =>
                  Exists.elim hu
                    (fun (m : Nat → ratRep)
                        (hm : ∃ (v : Nat → ratRep),
                          LowerStep a b p f l ∧ UpperStep a b p f u ∧
                          LowerStep a b p g m ∧ UpperStep a b p g v ∧
                          ratLtRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l))
                            (ratHalf eps) ∧
                          ratLtRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p v) (stepSum p m))
                            (ratHalf eps)) =>
                      Exists.elim hm
                        (fun (v : Nat → ratRep)
                            (hv : LowerStep a b p f l ∧
                              UpperStep a b p f u ∧
                              LowerStep a b p g m ∧
                              UpperStep a b p g v ∧
                              ratLtRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l))
                                (ratHalf eps) ∧
                              ratLtRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p v) (stepSum p m))
                                (ratHalf eps)) =>
                          Exists.intro p
                            (And.intro (And.left hp)
                              (Exists.intro
                                (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (l i) (m i))
                                (Exists.intro
                                  (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (u i) (v i))
                                  (And.intro
                                    (lowerStep_add a b p f g l m
                                      (And.left hv)
                                      (And.left
                                        (And.right (And.right hv))) )
                                    (And.intro
                                      (upperStep_add a b p f g u v
                                        (And.left (And.right hv))
                                        (And.left
                                          (And.right
                                            (And.right (And.right hv)))))
                                      (stepGap_add_lt p l u m v eps
                                        (And.left
                                          (And.right
                                            (And.right
                                              (And.right
                                                (And.right hv)))))
                                        (And.right
                                          (And.right
                                            (And.right
                                              (And.right
                                                (And.right hv)))))))))))))))))
练习 10demo_upperSum_add_const定理附录解答

两个常值函数

theorem demo_upperSum_add_const (a b k1 k2 : ratRep) :
    UpperSum a b (twoPoint a b) (fun (_ : ratRep) => ratAddRep k1 k2)
      (ratAddRep (ratMulRep k1 (ratSubRep b a))
        (ratMulRep k2 (ratSubRep b a))) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

两条常值曲线在两切点分割上的上和分别是 k1 · (b - a) 与 k2 · (b - a)；把 upperSum_add 用在它们上面，和就是常值 k1 + k2 的上和，值是两值相加。

提示 2目标形态

UpperSum a b (twoPoint a b) (fun _ => k1 + k2) (k1 · (b - a) + k2 · (b - a))。

提示 3关键件

upperSum_twoPoint_const · upperSum_add。

查看解答 定理 · 8 行

两条常值曲线的上和都已经有读数，把 upperSum_add 直接用在它们上面。

theorem demo_upperSum_add_const (a b k1 k2 : ratRep) :
    UpperSum a b (twoPoint a b) (fun (_ : ratRep) => ratAddRep k1 k2)
      (ratAddRep (ratMulRep k1 (ratSubRep b a))
        (ratMulRep k2 (ratSubRep b a))) :=
  upperSum_add a b (twoPoint a b)
    (fun (_ : ratRep) => k1) (fun (_ : ratRep) => k2)
    (ratMulRep k1 (ratSubRep b a)) (ratMulRep k2 (ratSubRep b a))
    (upperSum_twoPoint_const a b k1) (upperSum_twoPoint_const a b k2)

上和与下和的加法：先分开算再相加

10 道练习 · 10 份按名匹配的解答

练习 1 · segLen_mul_add 定理

宽写在右边，先交换成「宽乘高度和」。分配律把「宽乘和」拆成两个乘积， 再逐项交换回高度在前。两条交换加一次分配，就是这一段上的全部内容。

theorem segLen_mul_add (p : Partition) (i : Nat) (h1 h2 : ratRep) :
    ratRel (ratMulRep (ratAddRep h1 h2) (segLen p i))
      (ratAddRep (ratMulRep h1 (segLen p i)) (ratMulRep h2 (segLen p i))) :=
  ratRel_trans_gen
    (ratMulRep (ratAddRep h1 h2) (segLen p i))
    (ratMulRep (segLen p i) (ratAddRep h1 h2))
    (ratAddRep (ratMulRep h1 (segLen p i)) (ratMulRep h2 (segLen p i)))
    (ratMul_comm (ratAddRep h1 h2) (segLen p i))
    (ratRel_trans_gen
      (ratMulRep (segLen p i) (ratAddRep h1 h2))
      (ratAddRep (ratMulRep (segLen p i) h1) (ratMulRep (segLen p i) h2))
      (ratAddRep (ratMulRep h1 (segLen p i)) (ratMulRep h2 (segLen p i)))
      (ratMul_add (segLen p i) h1 h2)
      (ratRel_trans_gen
        (ratAddRep (ratMulRep (segLen p i) h1)
          (ratMulRep (segLen p i) h2))
        (ratAddRep (ratMulRep h1 (segLen p i))
          (ratMulRep (segLen p i) h2))
        (ratAddRep (ratMulRep h1 (segLen p i))
          (ratMulRep h2 (segLen p i)))
        (ratAddRep_wd_gen (ratMulRep (segLen p i) h1)
          (ratMulRep h1 (segLen p i)) (ratMulRep (segLen p i) h2)
          (ratMul_comm (segLen p i) h1))
        (ratAddRep_wd_snd_gen (ratMulRep h1 (segLen p i))
          (ratMulRep (segLen p i) h2) (ratMulRep h2 (segLen p i))
          (ratMul_comm (segLen p i) h2))))
练习 2 · stepSum_add 定理

和的每个矩形项都换成两个乘积之和，逐项相等把整条和搬过去；搬完的序列 正是「两摞高度各自的矩形和」逐项相加，有限和的加法律把它拆成两个和。

theorem stepSum_add (p : Partition) (u v : Nat → ratRep) :
    ratRel (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (u i) (v i)))
      (ratAddRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p v)) :=
  ratRel_trans_gen
    (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (u i) (v i)))
    (sumTo (fun (i : Nat) =>
        ratAddRep (ratMulRep (u i) (segLen p i))
          (ratMulRep (v i) (segLen p i))) (partCount p))
    (ratAddRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p v))
    (sumTo_congr
      (fun (i : Nat) => ratMulRep (ratAddRep (u i) (v i)) (segLen p i))
      (fun (i : Nat) =>
        ratAddRep (ratMulRep (u i) (segLen p i))
          (ratMulRep (v i) (segLen p i)))
      (partCount p)
      (fun (i : Nat) => segLen_mul_add p i (u i) (v i)))
    (sumTo_add
      (fun (i : Nat) => ratMulRep (u i) (segLen p i))
      (fun (i : Nat) => ratMulRep (v i) (segLen p i))
      (partCount p))
练习 3 · upperStep_add 定理

段与点都由同一份数据给出，两条假设在同一个 i、hi、x、hx 上成立； 加法保序把两个上界并成一个。

theorem upperStep_add (a b : ratRep) (p : Partition)
    (f g : ratRep → ratRep) (u v : Nat → ratRep)
    (hu : UpperStep a b p f u) (hv : UpperStep a b p g v) :
    UpperStep a b p (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f x) (g x))
      (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (u i) (v i)) :=
  fun (i : Nat) (hi : LeNat (Nat.succ i) (partCount p))
      (x : ratRep)
      (hx : InClosed (partPoint p i) (partPoint p (Nat.succ i)) x) =>
    ratLeRep_add (f x) (u i) (g x) (v i)
      (hu i hi x hx) (hv i hi x hx)
练习 4 · lowerStep_add 定理

下界的方向相同：两个下界各垫在自己那一项下面，加法保序把和垫在两者的和 下面。

theorem lowerStep_add (a b : ratRep) (p : Partition)
    (f g : ratRep → ratRep) (l m : Nat → ratRep)
    (hl : LowerStep a b p f l) (hm : LowerStep a b p g m) :
    LowerStep a b p (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f x) (g x))
      (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (l i) (m i)) :=
  fun (i : Nat) (hi : LeNat (Nat.succ i) (partCount p))
      (x : ratRep)
      (hx : InClosed (partPoint p i) (partPoint p (Nat.succ i)) x) =>
    ratLeRep_add (l i) (f x) (m i) (g x)
      (hl i hi x hx) (hm i hi x hx)
练习 5 · upperSum_add 定理

从两个上和里各取出一摞上界。逐段相加就是和的上界；矩形和按 stepSum_add 分成两个，候选值也随之相加。两处候选值都只是等价写法，用一次两边同加的 搬运把它们接到 U + V 上。

theorem upperSum_add (a b : ratRep) (p : Partition)
    (f g : ratRep → ratRep) (U V : ratRep)
    (hU : UpperSum a b p f U) (hV : UpperSum a b p g V) :
    UpperSum a b p (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f x) (g x))
      (ratAddRep U V) :=
  Exists.elim (upperSum_witness a b p f U hU)
    (fun (u : Nat → ratRep)
        (hu : UpperStep a b p f u ∧ ratRel U (stepSum p u)) =>
      Exists.elim (upperSum_witness a b p g V hV)
        (fun (v : Nat → ratRep)
            (hv : UpperStep a b p g v ∧ ratRel V (stepSum p v)) =>
          Exists.intro (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (u i) (v i))
            (And.intro
              (upperStep_add a b p f g u v (And.left hu) (And.left hv))
              (ratRel_trans_gen
                (ratAddRep U V)
                (ratAddRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p v))
                (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (u i) (v i)))
                (ratRel_trans_gen
                  (ratAddRep U V)
                  (ratAddRep (stepSum p u) V)
                  (ratAddRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p v))
                  (ratAddRep_wd_gen U (stepSum p u) V (And.right hu))
                  (ratAddRep_wd_snd_gen (stepSum p u) V (stepSum p v)
                    (And.right hv)))
                (ratRel_symm
                  (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (u i) (v i)))
                  (ratAddRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p v))
                  (stepSum_add p u v))))))
练习 6 · lowerSum_add 定理

下和同形：两摞下界逐段相加，矩形和分成两个，候选值相加。

theorem lowerSum_add (a b : ratRep) (p : Partition)
    (f g : ratRep → ratRep) (L M : ratRep)
    (hL : LowerSum a b p f L) (hM : LowerSum a b p g M) :
    LowerSum a b p (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f x) (g x))
      (ratAddRep L M) :=
  Exists.elim (lowerSum_witness a b p f L hL)
    (fun (l : Nat → ratRep)
        (hl : LowerStep a b p f l ∧ ratRel L (stepSum p l)) =>
      Exists.elim (lowerSum_witness a b p g M hM)
        (fun (m : Nat → ratRep)
            (hm : LowerStep a b p g m ∧ ratRel M (stepSum p m)) =>
          Exists.intro (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (l i) (m i))
            (And.intro
              (lowerStep_add a b p f g l m (And.left hl) (And.left hm))
              (ratRel_trans_gen
                (ratAddRep L M)
                (ratAddRep (stepSum p l) (stepSum p m))
                (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (l i) (m i)))
                (ratRel_trans_gen
                  (ratAddRep L M)
                  (ratAddRep (stepSum p l) M)
                  (ratAddRep (stepSum p l) (stepSum p m))
                  (ratAddRep_wd_gen L (stepSum p l) M (And.right hl))
                  (ratAddRep_wd_snd_gen (stepSum p l) M (stepSum p m)
                    (And.right hm)))
                (ratRel_symm
                  (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (l i) (m i)))
                  (ratAddRep (stepSum p l) (stepSum p m))
                  (stepSum_add p l m))))))
练习 7 · stepGap_add 定理

「两个差相加成一个大差」的反方向就是这一条：和的差拆成两个差的和。

theorem stepGap_add (p : Partition) (l u m v : Nat → ratRep) :
    ratRel
      (ratSubRep (ratAddRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p v))
        (ratAddRep (stepSum p l) (stepSum p m)))
      (ratAddRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l))
        (ratSubRep (stepSum p v) (stepSum p m))) :=
  ratRel_symm
    (ratAddRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l))
      (ratSubRep (stepSum p v) (stepSum p m)))
    (ratSubRep (ratAddRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p v))
      (ratAddRep (stepSum p l) (stepSum p m)))
    (ratAdd_sub_sub (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l) (stepSum p v)
      (stepSum p m))
练习 8 · stepGap_add_lt 定理

两个差相加先小于 ε/2 + ε/2，再沿「半数加半数等于 ε」把右端换成 ε。左边 是「两个差的和」，先把和的差拆成它，才能接上两个已知的严格界。

theorem stepGap_add_lt (p : Partition) (l u m v : Nat → ratRep)
    (eps : ratRep)
    (hf : ratLtRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l))
      (ratHalf eps))
    (hg : ratLtRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p v) (stepSum p m))
      (ratHalf eps)) :
    ratLtRep
      (ratSubRep (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (u i) (v i)))
        (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (l i) (m i)))) eps :=
  ratLtRep_congr_fst
    (ratSubRep (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (u i) (v i)))
      (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (l i) (m i))))
    (ratAddRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l))
      (ratSubRep (stepSum p v) (stepSum p m)))
    eps
    (ratLtRep_congr_snd
      (ratAddRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l))
        (ratSubRep (stepSum p v) (stepSum p m)))
      (ratAddRep (ratHalf eps) (ratHalf eps))
      eps
      (ratLtRep_add (ratSubRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l))
        (ratHalf eps) (ratSubRep (stepSum p v) (stepSum p m))
        (ratHalf eps) hf hg)
      (ratHalf_add_self eps))
    (ratRel_trans_gen
      (ratSubRep (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (u i) (v i)))
        (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (l i) (m i))))
      (ratSubRep (ratAddRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p v))
        (ratAddRep (stepSum p l) (stepSum p m)))
      (ratAddRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l))
        (ratSubRep (stepSum p v) (stepSum p m)))
      (ratSubRep_wd
        (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (u i) (v i)))
        (ratAddRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p v))
        (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (l i) (m i)))
        (ratAddRep (stepSum p l) (stepSum p m))
        (stepSum_add p u v) (stepSum_add p l m))
      (ratRel_symm
        (ratAddRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l))
          (ratSubRep (stepSum p v) (stepSum p m)))
        (ratSubRep (ratAddRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p v))
          (ratAddRep (stepSum p l) (stepSum p m)))
        (ratAdd_sub_sub (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l) (stepSum p v)
          (stepSum p m))))
练习 9 · integrable_add_of_gap 定理

判据要的是「每个正 ε 都有一套台阶」。假设正好给出这一套：分割、两条下界、 两条上界、两个差各自小于 ε/2。四条台阶逐段相加，得到和的下界与上界； 两个差由上一节压成和的一个差，仍然小于 ε。于是判据的另一个方向接上了。

theorem integrable_add_of_gap (a b : ratRep)
    (f g : ratRep → ratRep)
    (h : ∀ (eps : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep eps →
      ∃ (p : Partition), IsPartition a b p ∧
        ∃ (l : Nat → ratRep), ∃ (u : Nat → ratRep),
          ∃ (m : Nat → ratRep), ∃ (v : Nat → ratRep),
            LowerStep a b p f l ∧ UpperStep a b p f u ∧
            LowerStep a b p g m ∧ UpperStep a b p g v ∧
            ratLtRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l))
              (ratHalf eps) ∧
            ratLtRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p v) (stepSum p m))
              (ratHalf eps)) :
    Integrable a b (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f x) (g x)) :=
  integrable_of_gap a b (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f x) (g x))
    (fun (eps : ratRep) (heps : ratLtRep ratZeroRep eps) =>
      Exists.elim (h eps heps)
        (fun (p : Partition)
            (hp : IsPartition a b p ∧
              ∃ (l : Nat → ratRep), ∃ (u : Nat → ratRep),
                ∃ (m : Nat → ratRep), ∃ (v : Nat → ratRep),
                  LowerStep a b p f l ∧ UpperStep a b p f u ∧
                  LowerStep a b p g m ∧ UpperStep a b p g v ∧
                  ratLtRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l))
                    (ratHalf eps) ∧
                  ratLtRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p v) (stepSum p m))
                    (ratHalf eps)) =>
          Exists.elim (And.right hp)
            (fun (l : Nat → ratRep)
                (hl : ∃ (u : Nat → ratRep), ∃ (m : Nat → ratRep),
                  ∃ (v : Nat → ratRep),
                    LowerStep a b p f l ∧ UpperStep a b p f u ∧
                    LowerStep a b p g m ∧ UpperStep a b p g v ∧
                    ratLtRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l))
                      (ratHalf eps) ∧
                    ratLtRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p v) (stepSum p m))
                      (ratHalf eps)) =>
              Exists.elim hl
                (fun (u : Nat → ratRep)
                    (hu : ∃ (m : Nat → ratRep), ∃ (v : Nat → ratRep),
                      LowerStep a b p f l ∧ UpperStep a b p f u ∧
                      LowerStep a b p g m ∧ UpperStep a b p g v ∧
                      ratLtRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l))
                        (ratHalf eps) ∧
                      ratLtRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p v) (stepSum p m))
                        (ratHalf eps)) =>
                  Exists.elim hu
                    (fun (m : Nat → ratRep)
                        (hm : ∃ (v : Nat → ratRep),
                          LowerStep a b p f l ∧ UpperStep a b p f u ∧
                          LowerStep a b p g m ∧ UpperStep a b p g v ∧
                          ratLtRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l))
                            (ratHalf eps) ∧
                          ratLtRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p v) (stepSum p m))
                            (ratHalf eps)) =>
                      Exists.elim hm
                        (fun (v : Nat → ratRep)
                            (hv : LowerStep a b p f l ∧
                              UpperStep a b p f u ∧
                              LowerStep a b p g m ∧
                              UpperStep a b p g v ∧
                              ratLtRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l))
                                (ratHalf eps) ∧
                              ratLtRep (ratSubRep (stepSum p v) (stepSum p m))
                                (ratHalf eps)) =>
                          Exists.intro p
                            (And.intro (And.left hp)
                              (Exists.intro
                                (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (l i) (m i))
                                (Exists.intro
                                  (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (u i) (v i))
                                  (And.intro
                                    (lowerStep_add a b p f g l m
                                      (And.left hv)
                                      (And.left
                                        (And.right (And.right hv))) )
                                    (And.intro
                                      (upperStep_add a b p f g u v
                                        (And.left (And.right hv))
                                        (And.left
                                          (And.right
                                            (And.right (And.right hv)))))
                                      (stepGap_add_lt p l u m v eps
                                        (And.left
                                          (And.right
                                            (And.right
                                              (And.right
                                                (And.right hv)))))
                                        (And.right
                                          (And.right
                                            (And.right
                                              (And.right
                                                (And.right hv)))))))))))))))))
练习 10 · demo_upperSum_add_const 定理

两条常值曲线的上和都已经有读数，把 upperSum_add 直接用在它们上面。

theorem demo_upperSum_add_const (a b k1 k2 : ratRep) :
    UpperSum a b (twoPoint a b) (fun (_ : ratRep) => ratAddRep k1 k2)
      (ratAddRep (ratMulRep k1 (ratSubRep b a))
        (ratMulRep k2 (ratSubRep b a))) :=
  upperSum_add a b (twoPoint a b)
    (fun (_ : ratRep) => k1) (fun (_ : ratRep) => k2)
    (ratMulRep k1 (ratSubRep b a)) (ratMulRep k2 (ratSubRep b a))
    (upperSum_twoPoint_const a b k1) (upperSum_twoPoint_const a b k2)

III.8 · 本章第 12 / 16 单元 · 全书第 162 / 171 单元

162整条曲线抬高：面积差只认起伏

Unit 162 — Lifting the Whole Curve: The Area Gap Only Sees Oscillation

10 个定理 · 10 道练习 · 30 条提示 · 解答附录

↑ 目录

上一单元把两块台阶的面积分开算，但还没有处理一种最简单的变形：把整条曲线往上搬一段。搬动不改变曲线自己的起伏，只改变它离横轴的高度。上和与下和会各自多出什么？

练习 1segLen_mul_add_const定理附录解答

每一段上，上界和下界都抬高同一个常数 k。矩形的高多了一截，宽没有变，于是每一块矩形都多出「k 乘这一段的宽」。整条和因此多出 k 乘所有段宽之和，而所有段宽之和正好是区间长度 b - a。上和与下和抬高的正是同一个数。

两个和之差不变。面积估计只认曲线在一个区间里起伏多少，不认它整体挂得多高。常值曲线是最直接的读数——它的上和与下和都是 k·(b - a)，抬一段就把这个数加到两者头上。对一般的曲线，这条事实把常数项从面积估计里完全摘出去，后面谈线性时只剩函数本身的加法。

证明按四层走：一段上的分配律、矩形和、两摞台阶、两个和之差。每层只用乘法对加法的分配律、加法结合律和「减去同一个加数」，没有新的估计。

这里只处理垂直平移。一般的正数倍要把常数与 ε 放在一起比较，那要等一条有理数的阿基米德性质；这一步还没有做。函数取 ratRep → ratRep，停在有理代表元层。

一段上高度加常数

theorem segLen_mul_add_const (p : Partition) (i : Nat) (h k : ratRep) :
    ratRel (ratMulRep (ratAddRep h k) (segLen p i))
      (ratAddRep (ratMulRep h (segLen p i))
        (ratMulRep k (segLen p i))) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

先把「高加常数再乘宽」交换成「宽乘高加常数」，用分配律拆成两个乘积，再逐项交换回高度在前。两条交换与一次分配就是全部内容。

提示 2目标形态

ratRel (ratMulRep (ratAddRep h k) (segLen p i)) (ratAddRep (ratMulRep h (segLen p i)) (ratMulRep k (segLen p i)))。

提示 3关键件

ratMul_comm · ratMul_add · ratAddRep_wd_gen · ratAddRep_wd_snd_gen。

查看解答 定理 · 30 行

宽在右边，先交换成「宽乘高度和」，分配律拆成两个乘积，再逐项交换回 高度在前。

theorem segLen_mul_add_const (p : Partition) (i : Nat) (h k : ratRep) :
    ratRel (ratMulRep (ratAddRep h k) (segLen p i))
      (ratAddRep (ratMulRep h (segLen p i))
        (ratMulRep k (segLen p i))) :=
  ratRel_trans_gen
    (ratMulRep (ratAddRep h k) (segLen p i))
    (ratMulRep (segLen p i) (ratAddRep h k))
    (ratAddRep (ratMulRep h (segLen p i))
      (ratMulRep k (segLen p i)))
    (ratMul_comm (ratAddRep h k) (segLen p i))
    (ratRel_trans_gen
      (ratMulRep (segLen p i) (ratAddRep h k))
      (ratAddRep (ratMulRep (segLen p i) h)
        (ratMulRep (segLen p i) k))
      (ratAddRep (ratMulRep h (segLen p i))
        (ratMulRep k (segLen p i)))
      (ratMul_add (segLen p i) h k)
      (ratRel_trans_gen
        (ratAddRep (ratMulRep (segLen p i) h)
          (ratMulRep (segLen p i) k))
        (ratAddRep (ratMulRep h (segLen p i))
          (ratMulRep (segLen p i) k))
        (ratAddRep (ratMulRep h (segLen p i))
          (ratMulRep k (segLen p i)))
        (ratAddRep_wd_gen (ratMulRep (segLen p i) h)
          (ratMulRep h (segLen p i)) (ratMulRep (segLen p i) k)
          (ratMul_comm (segLen p i) h))
        (ratAddRep_wd_snd_gen (ratMulRep h (segLen p i))
          (ratMulRep (segLen p i) k) (ratMulRep k (segLen p i))
          (ratMul_comm (segLen p i) k))))
练习 2stepSum_add_const定理附录解答

矩形和抬高常数乘区间长度

theorem stepSum_add_const (a b : ratRep) (p : Partition)
    (hp : IsPartition a b p) (h : Nat → ratRep) (k : ratRep) :
    ratRel (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (h i) k))
      (ratAddRep (stepSum p h) (ratMulRep k (ratSubRep b a))) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

每一项按上一条拆成两块矩形；有限和按函数分成两摞，第一摞是原来的矩形和，第二摞是常值乘宽的和，由常值面积读数收成 k·(b - a)。

提示 2目标形态

ratRel (stepSum p (fun i => h i + k)) (ratAddRep (stepSum p h) (ratMulRep k (ratSubRep b a)))。

提示 3关键件

sumTo_congr · segLen_mul_add_const · sumTo_add · stepSum_const。

查看解答 定理 · 34 行

逐项拆开，有限和按函数分成两摞；第二摞是常值乘宽的和，由常值面积读数 收成 k·(b - a)。

theorem stepSum_add_const (a b : ratRep) (p : Partition)
    (hp : IsPartition a b p) (h : Nat → ratRep) (k : ratRep) :
    ratRel (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (h i) k))
      (ratAddRep (stepSum p h) (ratMulRep k (ratSubRep b a))) :=
  ratRel_trans_gen
    (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (h i) k))
    (sumTo (fun (i : Nat) =>
      ratAddRep (ratMulRep (h i) (segLen p i))
        (ratMulRep k (segLen p i))) (partCount p))
    (ratAddRep (stepSum p h) (ratMulRep k (ratSubRep b a)))
    (sumTo_congr (fun (i : Nat) =>
        ratMulRep (ratAddRep (h i) k) (segLen p i))
      (fun (i : Nat) =>
        ratAddRep (ratMulRep (h i) (segLen p i))
          (ratMulRep k (segLen p i)))
      (partCount p)
      (fun (i : Nat) => segLen_mul_add_const p i (h i) k))
    (ratRel_trans_gen
      (sumTo (fun (i : Nat) =>
        ratAddRep (ratMulRep (h i) (segLen p i))
          (ratMulRep k (segLen p i))) (partCount p))
      (ratAddRep
        (sumTo (fun (i : Nat) => ratMulRep (h i) (segLen p i))
          (partCount p))
        (sumTo (fun (i : Nat) => ratMulRep k (segLen p i))
          (partCount p)))
      (ratAddRep (stepSum p h) (ratMulRep k (ratSubRep b a)))
      (sumTo_add (fun (i : Nat) => ratMulRep (h i) (segLen p i))
        (fun (i : Nat) => ratMulRep k (segLen p i)) (partCount p))

        (sumTo (fun (i : Nat) => ratMulRep k (segLen p i))
          (partCount p))

        (stepSum_const a b p hp k)))

上和与下和各抬一截

theorem upperSum_add_const (a b : ratRep) (p : Partition)
    (f : ratRep → ratRep) (U : ratRep) (k : ratRep)

查看解答 定理 · 21 行

从上和里取出那摞上界，整体抬高；候选值同时抬高 k·(b - a)。

theorem upperSum_add_const (a b : ratRep) (p : Partition)
    (f : ratRep → ratRep) (U : ratRep) (k : ratRep)
    (hp : IsPartition a b p) (h : UpperSum a b p f U) :
    UpperSum a b p (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f x) k)
      (ratAddRep U (ratMulRep k (ratSubRep b a))) :=
  Exists.elim (upperSum_witness a b p f U h)
    (fun (u : Nat → ratRep)
        (hu : UpperStep a b p f u ∧ ratRel U (stepSum p u)) =>
      Exists.intro (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (u i) k)
        (And.intro
          (upperStep_add_const a b p f u k (And.left hu))
          (ratRel_trans_gen
            (ratAddRep U (ratMulRep k (ratSubRep b a)))
            (ratAddRep (stepSum p u) (ratMulRep k (ratSubRep b a)))
            (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (u i) k))
            (ratAddRep_wd_gen U (stepSum p u)
              (ratMulRep k (ratSubRep b a)) (And.right hu))
            (ratRel_symm
              (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (u i) k))
              (ratAddRep (stepSum p u) (ratMulRep k (ratSubRep b a)))
              (stepSum_add_const a b p hp u k)))))

查看解答 定理 · 21 行

下和同形，把下界那摞整体抬高。

theorem lowerSum_add_const (a b : ratRep) (p : Partition)
    (f : ratRep → ratRep) (L : ratRep) (k : ratRep)
    (hp : IsPartition a b p) (h : LowerSum a b p f L) :
    LowerSum a b p (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f x) k)
      (ratAddRep L (ratMulRep k (ratSubRep b a))) :=
  Exists.elim (lowerSum_witness a b p f L h)
    (fun (l : Nat → ratRep)
        (hl : LowerStep a b p f l ∧ ratRel L (stepSum p l)) =>
      Exists.intro (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (l i) k)
        (And.intro
          (lowerStep_add_const a b p f l k (And.left hl))
          (ratRel_trans_gen
            (ratAddRep L (ratMulRep k (ratSubRep b a)))
            (ratAddRep (stepSum p l) (ratMulRep k (ratSubRep b a)))
            (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (l i) k))
            (ratAddRep_wd_gen L (stepSum p l)
              (ratMulRep k (ratSubRep b a)) (And.right hl))
            (ratRel_symm
              (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (l i) k))
              (ratAddRep (stepSum p l) (ratMulRep k (ratSubRep b a)))
              (stepSum_add_const a b p hp l k)))))
练习 7stepGap_add_const定理附录解答

两个和之差不变

theorem stepGap_add_const (p : Partition) (L U k : ratRep) :
    ratRel (ratSubRep (ratAddRep U k) (ratAddRep L k))
      (ratSubRep U L) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

两个和都加了同一个常数，差里它只出现在被减数与减数各一次；消去公共加数就把这一截拿掉。

theorem stepGap_add_const (p : Partition) (L U k : ratRep) :
    ratRel (ratSubRep (ratAddRep U k) (ratAddRep L k))
      (ratSubRep U L) :=
  ratSubRep_add_cancel U L k
练习 8integrable_add_const定理附录解答

可积性在垂直平移下不变

theorem integrable_add_const (a b : ratRep) (f : ratRep → ratRep)
    (k : ratRep) (h : Integrable a b f) :
    Integrable a b (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f x) k) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

给定 ε，取原来的分割与两个和，两个都抬高 k·(b - a)；差由 stepGap_add_const 换回原来的差，仍然小于 ε。

提示 2目标形态

Integrable a b (fun x => f x + k)。

提示 3关键件

lowerSum_add_const · upperSum_add_const · stepGap_add_const · ratLtRep_congr_fst。

查看解答 定理 · 38 行

两个和都抬高 k·(b - a)，差换回原来的差。

theorem integrable_add_const (a b : ratRep) (f : ratRep → ratRep)
    (k : ratRep) (h : Integrable a b f) :
    Integrable a b (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f x) k) :=
  fun (eps : ratRep) (heps : ratLtRep ratZeroRep eps) =>
    Exists.elim (h eps heps)
      (fun (p : Partition)
          (hp : IsPartition a b p ∧
            ∃ (L : ratRep), ∃ (U : ratRep),
              LowerSum a b p f L ∧ UpperSum a b p f U ∧
                ratLtRep (ratSubRep U L) eps) =>
        Exists.elim (And.right hp)
          (fun (L : ratRep) (hL : ∃ (U : ratRep),
              LowerSum a b p f L ∧ UpperSum a b p f U ∧
                ratLtRep (ratSubRep U L) eps) =>
            Exists.elim hL
              (fun (U : ratRep)
                  (hU : LowerSum a b p f L ∧ UpperSum a b p f U ∧
                    ratLtRep (ratSubRep U L) eps) =>
                Exists.intro p
                  (And.intro (And.left hp)
                    (Exists.intro
                      (ratAddRep L (ratMulRep k (ratSubRep b a)))
                      (Exists.intro
                        (ratAddRep U (ratMulRep k (ratSubRep b a)))
                        (And.intro
                          (lowerSum_add_const a b p f L k
                            (And.left hp) (And.left hU))
                          (And.intro
                            (upperSum_add_const a b p f U k
                              (And.left hp) (And.left (And.right hU)))
                            (ratLtRep_congr_fst
                              (ratSubRep
                                (ratAddRep U (ratMulRep k (ratSubRep b a)))
                                (ratAddRep L (ratMulRep k (ratSubRep b a))))
                              (ratSubRep U L) eps
                              (And.right (And.right hU))
                              (stepGap_add_const p L U
                                (ratMulRep k (ratSubRep b a))))))))))))
练习 9integrable_add_const_iff定理附录解答
theorem integrable_add_const_iff (a b : ratRep) (f : ratRep → ratRep)
    (k : ratRep) :
    Integrable a b f ↔ Integrable a b (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f x) k) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

反方向把 f + k 再抬高 -k，得到 (f + k) + (-k)；它与 f 逐点相等，用可积性沿点式等价搬运收回来。

提示 2目标形态

Integrable a b f ↔ Integrable a b (fun x => f x + k)。

提示 3关键件

integrable_add_const · integrable_congr_fun · ratAdd_assoc · ratAdd_neg_gen · ratAdd_zero。

查看解答 定理 · 27 行

反方向把 f + k 再抬高 -k，点式相等把它换回 f。

theorem integrable_add_const_iff (a b : ratRep) (f : ratRep → ratRep)
    (k : ratRep) :
    Integrable a b f ↔ Integrable a b (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f x) k) :=
  Iff.intro
    (integrable_add_const a b f k)
    (fun (h : Integrable a b (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f x) k)) =>
      Iff.mp
        (integrable_congr_fun a b
          (fun (x : ratRep) =>
            ratAddRep (ratAddRep (f x) k) (ratNegRep k))
          f
          (fun (x : ratRep) =>
            ratRel_trans_gen
              (ratAddRep (ratAddRep (f x) k) (ratNegRep k))
              (ratAddRep (f x) (ratAddRep k (ratNegRep k)))
              (f x)
              (ratAdd_assoc (f x) k (ratNegRep k))
              (ratRel_trans_gen
                (ratAddRep (f x) (ratAddRep k (ratNegRep k)))
                (ratAddRep (f x) ratZeroRep)
                (f x)
                (ratAddRep_wd_snd_gen (f x)
                  (ratAddRep k (ratNegRep k)) ratZeroRep
                  (ratAdd_neg_gen k))
                (ratAdd_zero (f x)))))
        (integrable_add_const a b
          (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f x) k) (ratNegRep k) h))
练习 10demo_upperSum_shift_const定理附录解答

常值曲线的读数

theorem demo_upperSum_shift_const (a b k0 k : ratRep)
    (hab : ratLtRep a b) :
    UpperSum a b (twoPoint a b) (fun (_ : ratRep) => ratAddRep k0 k)
      (ratAddRep (ratMulRep k0 (ratSubRep b a))
        (ratMulRep k (ratSubRep b a))) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

常值 k0 在两切点分割上的上和是 k0·(b - a)；把它抬高常数 k，用上和抬高的那条引理，候选值多出 k·(b - a)。

提示 2目标形态

查看解答 定理 · 9 行

theorem demo_upperSum_shift_const (a b k0 k : ratRep)
    (hab : ratLtRep a b) :
    UpperSum a b (twoPoint a b) (fun (_ : ratRep) => ratAddRep k0 k)
      (ratAddRep (ratMulRep k0 (ratSubRep b a))
        (ratMulRep k (ratSubRep b a))) :=
  upperSum_add_const a b (twoPoint a b) (fun (_ : ratRep) => k0)
    (ratMulRep k0 (ratSubRep b a)) k
    (twoPoint_isPartition a b hab)
    (upperSum_twoPoint_const a b k0)

整条曲线抬高：面积差只认起伏

10 道练习 · 10 份按名匹配的解答 · 3 个解答独有声明

练习 1 · segLen_mul_add_const 定理

宽在右边，先交换成「宽乘高度和」，分配律拆成两个乘积，再逐项交换回 高度在前。

theorem segLen_mul_add_const (p : Partition) (i : Nat) (h k : ratRep) :
    ratRel (ratMulRep (ratAddRep h k) (segLen p i))
      (ratAddRep (ratMulRep h (segLen p i))
        (ratMulRep k (segLen p i))) :=
  ratRel_trans_gen
    (ratMulRep (ratAddRep h k) (segLen p i))
    (ratMulRep (segLen p i) (ratAddRep h k))
    (ratAddRep (ratMulRep h (segLen p i))
      (ratMulRep k (segLen p i)))
    (ratMul_comm (ratAddRep h k) (segLen p i))
    (ratRel_trans_gen
      (ratMulRep (segLen p i) (ratAddRep h k))
      (ratAddRep (ratMulRep (segLen p i) h)
        (ratMulRep (segLen p i) k))
      (ratAddRep (ratMulRep h (segLen p i))
        (ratMulRep k (segLen p i)))
      (ratMul_add (segLen p i) h k)
      (ratRel_trans_gen
        (ratAddRep (ratMulRep (segLen p i) h)
          (ratMulRep (segLen p i) k))
        (ratAddRep (ratMulRep h (segLen p i))
          (ratMulRep (segLen p i) k))
        (ratAddRep (ratMulRep h (segLen p i))
          (ratMulRep k (segLen p i)))
        (ratAddRep_wd_gen (ratMulRep (segLen p i) h)
          (ratMulRep h (segLen p i)) (ratMulRep (segLen p i) k)
          (ratMul_comm (segLen p i) h))
        (ratAddRep_wd_snd_gen (ratMulRep h (segLen p i))
          (ratMulRep (segLen p i) k) (ratMulRep k (segLen p i))
          (ratMul_comm (segLen p i) k))))
练习 2 · stepSum_add_const 定理

逐项拆开，有限和按函数分成两摞；第二摞是常值乘宽的和，由常值面积读数 收成 k·(b - a)。

theorem stepSum_add_const (a b : ratRep) (p : Partition)
    (hp : IsPartition a b p) (h : Nat → ratRep) (k : ratRep) :
    ratRel (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (h i) k))
      (ratAddRep (stepSum p h) (ratMulRep k (ratSubRep b a))) :=
  ratRel_trans_gen
    (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (h i) k))
    (sumTo (fun (i : Nat) =>
      ratAddRep (ratMulRep (h i) (segLen p i))
        (ratMulRep k (segLen p i))) (partCount p))
    (ratAddRep (stepSum p h) (ratMulRep k (ratSubRep b a)))
    (sumTo_congr (fun (i : Nat) =>
        ratMulRep (ratAddRep (h i) k) (segLen p i))
      (fun (i : Nat) =>
        ratAddRep (ratMulRep (h i) (segLen p i))
          (ratMulRep k (segLen p i)))
      (partCount p)
      (fun (i : Nat) => segLen_mul_add_const p i (h i) k))
    (ratRel_trans_gen
      (sumTo (fun (i : Nat) =>
        ratAddRep (ratMulRep (h i) (segLen p i))
          (ratMulRep k (segLen p i))) (partCount p))
      (ratAddRep
        (sumTo (fun (i : Nat) => ratMulRep (h i) (segLen p i))
          (partCount p))
        (sumTo (fun (i : Nat) => ratMulRep k (segLen p i))
          (partCount p)))
      (ratAddRep (stepSum p h) (ratMulRep k (ratSubRep b a)))
      (sumTo_add (fun (i : Nat) => ratMulRep (h i) (segLen p i))
        (fun (i : Nat) => ratMulRep k (segLen p i)) (partCount p))
      (ratAddRep_wd_snd_gen (stepSum p h)
        (sumTo (fun (i : Nat) => ratMulRep k (segLen p i))
          (partCount p))
        (ratMulRep k (ratSubRep b a))
        (stepSum_const a b p hp k)))

练习 5 · upperSum_add_const 定理

从上和里取出那摞上界，整体抬高；候选值同时抬高 k·(b - a)。

theorem upperSum_add_const (a b : ratRep) (p : Partition)
    (f : ratRep → ratRep) (U : ratRep) (k : ratRep)
    (hp : IsPartition a b p) (h : UpperSum a b p f U) :
    UpperSum a b p (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f x) k)
      (ratAddRep U (ratMulRep k (ratSubRep b a))) :=
  Exists.elim (upperSum_witness a b p f U h)
    (fun (u : Nat → ratRep)
        (hu : UpperStep a b p f u ∧ ratRel U (stepSum p u)) =>
      Exists.intro (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (u i) k)
        (And.intro
          (upperStep_add_const a b p f u k (And.left hu))
          (ratRel_trans_gen
            (ratAddRep U (ratMulRep k (ratSubRep b a)))
            (ratAddRep (stepSum p u) (ratMulRep k (ratSubRep b a)))
            (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (u i) k))
            (ratAddRep_wd_gen U (stepSum p u)
              (ratMulRep k (ratSubRep b a)) (And.right hu))
            (ratRel_symm
              (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (u i) k))
              (ratAddRep (stepSum p u) (ratMulRep k (ratSubRep b a)))
              (stepSum_add_const a b p hp u k)))))
练习 6 · lowerSum_add_const 定理

下和同形，把下界那摞整体抬高。

theorem lowerSum_add_const (a b : ratRep) (p : Partition)
    (f : ratRep → ratRep) (L : ratRep) (k : ratRep)
    (hp : IsPartition a b p) (h : LowerSum a b p f L) :
    LowerSum a b p (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f x) k)
      (ratAddRep L (ratMulRep k (ratSubRep b a))) :=
  Exists.elim (lowerSum_witness a b p f L h)
    (fun (l : Nat → ratRep)
        (hl : LowerStep a b p f l ∧ ratRel L (stepSum p l)) =>
      Exists.intro (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (l i) k)
        (And.intro
          (lowerStep_add_const a b p f l k (And.left hl))
          (ratRel_trans_gen
            (ratAddRep L (ratMulRep k (ratSubRep b a)))
            (ratAddRep (stepSum p l) (ratMulRep k (ratSubRep b a)))
            (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (l i) k))
            (ratAddRep_wd_gen L (stepSum p l)
              (ratMulRep k (ratSubRep b a)) (And.right hl))
            (ratRel_symm
              (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => ratAddRep (l i) k))
              (ratAddRep (stepSum p l) (ratMulRep k (ratSubRep b a)))
              (stepSum_add_const a b p hp l k)))))
练习 7 · stepGap_add_const 定理

(U + K) - (L + K) 与 U - L 只差一次消去公共加数。

theorem stepGap_add_const (p : Partition) (L U k : ratRep) :
    ratRel (ratSubRep (ratAddRep U k) (ratAddRep L k))
      (ratSubRep U L) :=
  ratSubRep_add_cancel U L k
练习 8 · integrable_add_const 定理

两个和都抬高 k·(b - a)，差换回原来的差。

theorem integrable_add_const (a b : ratRep) (f : ratRep → ratRep)
    (k : ratRep) (h : Integrable a b f) :
    Integrable a b (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f x) k) :=
  fun (eps : ratRep) (heps : ratLtRep ratZeroRep eps) =>
    Exists.elim (h eps heps)
      (fun (p : Partition)
          (hp : IsPartition a b p ∧
            ∃ (L : ratRep), ∃ (U : ratRep),
              LowerSum a b p f L ∧ UpperSum a b p f U ∧
                ratLtRep (ratSubRep U L) eps) =>
        Exists.elim (And.right hp)
          (fun (L : ratRep) (hL : ∃ (U : ratRep),
              LowerSum a b p f L ∧ UpperSum a b p f U ∧
                ratLtRep (ratSubRep U L) eps) =>
            Exists.elim hL
              (fun (U : ratRep)
                  (hU : LowerSum a b p f L ∧ UpperSum a b p f U ∧
                    ratLtRep (ratSubRep U L) eps) =>
                Exists.intro p
                  (And.intro (And.left hp)
                    (Exists.intro
                      (ratAddRep L (ratMulRep k (ratSubRep b a)))
                      (Exists.intro
                        (ratAddRep U (ratMulRep k (ratSubRep b a)))
                        (And.intro
                          (lowerSum_add_const a b p f L k
                            (And.left hp) (And.left hU))
                          (And.intro
                            (upperSum_add_const a b p f U k
                              (And.left hp) (And.left (And.right hU)))
                            (ratLtRep_congr_fst
                              (ratSubRep
                                (ratAddRep U (ratMulRep k (ratSubRep b a)))
                                (ratAddRep L (ratMulRep k (ratSubRep b a))))
                              (ratSubRep U L) eps
                              (And.right (And.right hU))
                              (stepGap_add_const p L U
                                (ratMulRep k (ratSubRep b a))))))))))))
练习 9 · integrable_add_const_iff 定理

反方向把 f + k 再抬高 -k，点式相等把它换回 f。

theorem integrable_add_const_iff (a b : ratRep) (f : ratRep → ratRep)
    (k : ratRep) :
    Integrable a b f ↔ Integrable a b (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f x) k) :=
  Iff.intro
    (integrable_add_const a b f k)
    (fun (h : Integrable a b (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f x) k)) =>
      Iff.mp
        (integrable_congr_fun a b
          (fun (x : ratRep) =>
            ratAddRep (ratAddRep (f x) k) (ratNegRep k))
          f
          (fun (x : ratRep) =>
            ratRel_trans_gen
              (ratAddRep (ratAddRep (f x) k) (ratNegRep k))
              (ratAddRep (f x) (ratAddRep k (ratNegRep k)))
              (f x)
              (ratAdd_assoc (f x) k (ratNegRep k))
              (ratRel_trans_gen
                (ratAddRep (f x) (ratAddRep k (ratNegRep k)))
                (ratAddRep (f x) ratZeroRep)
                (f x)
                (ratAddRep_wd_snd_gen (f x)
                  (ratAddRep k (ratNegRep k)) ratZeroRep
                  (ratAdd_neg_gen k))
                (ratAdd_zero (f x)))))
        (integrable_add_const a b
          (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f x) k) (ratNegRep k) h))
练习 10 · demo_upperSum_shift_const 定理

常值 k0 在两切点分割上的上和给出 k0·(b - a)，抬高常数 k 后候选值 多出 k·(b - a)。

theorem demo_upperSum_shift_const (a b k0 k : ratRep)
    (hab : ratLtRep a b) :
    UpperSum a b (twoPoint a b) (fun (_ : ratRep) => ratAddRep k0 k)
      (ratAddRep (ratMulRep k0 (ratSubRep b a))

  upperSum_add_const a b (twoPoint a b) (fun (_ : ratRep) => k0)
    (ratMulRep k0 (ratSubRep b a)) k
    (twoPoint_isPartition a b hab)
    (upperSum_twoPoint_const a b k0)

theorem stepSum_const (a b : ratRep) (p : Partition) (hp : IsPartition a b p)

III.8 · 本章第 13 / 16 单元 · 全书第 163 / 171 单元

163阿基米德性质：反复相加总能超过

Unit 163 — The Archimedean Property: Repeated Addition Eventually Wins

1 个定义 · 11 个定理 · 7 道练习 · 21 条提示 · 解答附录

↑ 目录

要把面积误差压到 ε 以下，靠的是把区间切得足够细。切得够不够细，取决于同一个问题：一个正数反复相加，能不能超过区间长度？如果无论加多少次都超不过 b - a，那么段数再多也只能覆盖很窄的一段，误差压不下去。

在有理数里这件事可以直接算。一个正数写成 p − qm+1，其中 p > q；把它乘上 (r+1)(m+1)，分式变成 (r+1)(p - q)，而任何待超过的量 r/(j+1) 都不超过 r。倍数取得够大，就一定超过。

要把这句话写成证明，第一步是把「一个有理数不超过另一个」翻译成整数乘法的大小比较。交叉相乘之后，两个分母各被消到一边，剩下两条 ℕ 上的不等式；这一步把分式问题变成算术问题。

第二步是 ℕ 上的估计。下面四条事实反复出现：一乘任何数还是它自己；某数加上一段不会变小；两个至少为 1 的数相乘仍然至少为 1；把一段乘到两边，大小关系不变。主定理取 n = (r+1)(m+1)，代进交叉相乘的形式后，待证的不等式变成「一个乘积不超过另一个乘积」，四项各交给上面的一条。

严格版本不必重新算：把待超过的量换成它加一，弱序就收紧成严格序。

这条性质只管倍数能超过，不谈倒数，也不谈倍数能任意接近某个数；证明停在有理代表元层。

把正数写成 n/1 的倍数

自然数放进有理数：n/1。

练习 1ratLeRep_zero_lit定理附录解答

把「0 ≤ x」化成整数的大小

theorem ratLeRep_zero_lit (a b m : Nat) :
    ratLeRep ratZeroRep (qrep a b m) ↔ LeNat b a :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

把 ratLeRep 展开成 leRep (leNum …) (leNum …)，再用两条已给的交叉相乘公式把两个 leNum 换成 (0,0) 与 (a,b)；剩下的 0 + b ≤ a + 0 用 zero_add 与 add_zero 消掉。

theorem ratLeRep_zero_lit (a b m : Nat) :
    ratLeRep ratZeroRep (qrep a b m) ↔ LeNat b a :=
  Iff.intro
    (fun (h : ratLeRep ratZeroRep (qrep a b m)) =>
      @Eq.subst.{1} Nat
        (fun (z : Nat) => LeNat b z)
        (Nat.add a Nat.zero) a (add_zero a)

练习 4arch_le_lit定理附录解答

阿基米德性质

theorem arch_le_lit (p q m : Nat) (hpq : ratLtRep ratZeroRep (qrep p q m))
    (r s j : Nat) (hc : ratLeRep ratZeroRep (qrep r s j)) :
    ∃ (n : Nat), ratLeRep (qrep r s j)
      (ratMulRep (natToRat n) (qrep p q m)) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

由正性取 p = q + (k+1)，由非负取 r = s + u；倍数取 n = (r+1)(m+1)。代进交叉相乘的形式后，n·p 拆成 n·q + n·(k+1)，r 拆成 s + u，公共项两两对齐，剩下的 u(m+1) ≤ n(k+1)(j+1) 由「u ≤ r < r+1 ≤ n」与「两个至少为 1 的因子」两步乘出来。

提示 2目标形态

∃ n, ratLeRep (qrep r s j) (ratMulRep (natToRat n) (qrep p q m))。

提示 3关键件

ratLtRep_zero_num · ratLeRep_zero_lit · leNat_exists_add · mul_add · mul_add_right · succ_mul · leNat_mul_right · leNat_trans · ratLeRep_lit。

查看解答 定理 · 33 行

字面形式：0 < (p - q)/(m+1) 且 0 ≤ (r - s)/(j+1) 时，取 n = (r+1)(m+1) 就有 (r-s)/(j+1) ≤ n·(p-q)/(m+1)。

theorem arch_le_lit (p q m : Nat) (hpq : ratLtRep ratZeroRep (qrep p q m))
    (r s j : Nat) (hc : ratLeRep ratZeroRep (qrep r s j)) :
    ∃ (n : Nat), ratLeRep (qrep r s j)
      (ratMulRep (natToRat n) (qrep p q m)) :=
  Exists.elim
    (leNat_exists_add (Nat.succ q) p (ratLtRep_zero_num p q m hpq))
    (fun (k : Nat) (hk : p = Nat.add (Nat.succ q) k) =>
      Exists.elim
        (leNat_exists_add s r (Iff.mp (ratLeRep_zero_lit r s j) hc))
        (fun (u : Nat) (hu : r = Nat.add s u) =>
          Exists.intro (myMul (Nat.succ r) (Nat.succ m))
            (@Eq.subst.{1} ratRep
              (fun (z : ratRep) => ratLeRep (qrep r s j) z)
              (qrep
                (myMul (myMul (Nat.succ r) (Nat.succ m)) p)
                (myMul (myMul (Nat.succ r) (Nat.succ m)) q)
                m)
              (ratMulRep (natToRat (myMul (Nat.succ r) (Nat.succ m)))
                (qrep p q m))
              (@Eq.symm.{1} ratRep
                (ratMulRep (natToRat (myMul (Nat.succ r) (Nat.succ m)))
                  (qrep p q m))
                (qrep
                  (myMul (myMul (Nat.succ r) (Nat.succ m)) p)
                  (myMul (myMul (Nat.succ r) (Nat.succ m)) q)
                  m)
                (natToRat_mul_rep (myMul (Nat.succ r) (Nat.succ m)) p q m))
              (Iff.mpr
                (ratLeRep_lit r s j
                  (myMul (myMul (Nat.succ r) (Nat.succ m)) p)
                  (myMul (myMul (Nat.succ r) (Nat.succ m)) q)
                  m)
                (arch_G p q m r s u k j hk hu)))))
练习 5arch_le定理附录解答
theorem arch_le (x c : Prod (Prod Nat Nat) Nat) :
    ratLtRep ratZeroRep x → ratLeRep ratZeroRep c →
      ∃ (n : Nat), ratLeRep c (ratMulRep (natToRat n) x) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

任意代表元是三个分量，按「分母字段在外、分子序对在内」两层 match 拆开，两条前提的类型跟着变成字面形式，直接交给字面版本。前提要写在 match 里面的 fun 上，否则内核看不到替换后的形状。

提示 2目标形态

ratLtRep ratZeroRep x → ratLeRep ratZeroRep c → ∃ n, ratLeRep c (ratMulRep (natToRat n) x)。

提示 3关键件

arch_le_lit · 两层 match · 前提写成 fun 的绑定点。

查看解答 定理 · 10 行

任意代表元：两条前提换成「正」与「非负」，直接交给字面形式。

theorem arch_le (x c : Prod (Prod Nat Nat) Nat) :
    ratLtRep ratZeroRep x → ratLeRep ratZeroRep c →
      ∃ (n : Nat), ratLeRep c (ratMulRep (natToRat n) x) :=
  match x with
  | prod_mk (prod_mk p q) m =>
    match c with
    | prod_mk (prod_mk r s) j =>
      fun (hx : ratLtRep ratZeroRep (qrep p q m))
          (hc : ratLeRep ratZeroRep (qrep r s j)) =>
        arch_le_lit p q m hx r s j hc

theorem arch_lt_of_nonneg (x c : ratRep) (hx : ratLtRep ratZeroRep x)
    (hc : ratLeRep ratZeroRep c) :
    ∃ (n : Nat), ratLtRep c (ratMulRep (natToRat n) x) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

把待超过的量换成 c + 1 再取倍数，得到 c + 1 ≤ n·x；另一侧 c < c + 1 由「0 < 1 加在右边」得到，两条一夹就是 c < n·x。

提示 2目标形态

∃ n, ratLtRep c (ratMulRep (natToRat n) x)。

提示 3关键件

arch_le · ratLeRep_zero_add_nonneg · ratLtRep_zero_one · ratLtRep_self_add_pos · ratLtRep_of_lt_le。

查看解答 定理 · 14 行

严格版本：把 c 换成 c + 1 再取同一个倍数。

theorem arch_lt_of_nonneg (x c : ratRep) (hx : ratLtRep ratZeroRep x)
    (hc : ratLeRep ratZeroRep c) :
    ∃ (n : Nat), ratLtRep c (ratMulRep (natToRat n) x) :=
  Exists.elim
    (arch_le x (ratAddRep c ratOneRep) hx
      (ratLeRep_zero_add_nonneg c ratOneRep hc
        (ratLtRep_le ratZeroRep ratOneRep ratLtRep_zero_one)))
    (fun (n : Nat)
        (hn : ratLeRep (ratAddRep c ratOneRep) (ratMulRep (natToRat n) x)) =>
      Exists.intro n
        (ratLtRep_of_lt_le c (ratAddRep c ratOneRep)
          (ratMulRep (natToRat n) x)
          (ratLtRep_self_add_pos c ratOneRep ratLtRep_zero_one)
          hn))

提示 1思路

待证形式换成字面比较：左边是 1/1，右边是 2·1/2；交叉相乘后两边都是 2，交给自反的弱序。

提示 2目标形态

ratLeRep ratOneRep (ratMulRep (natToRat 2) (qrep 1 Nat.zero (Nat.succ Nat.zero)))。

提示 3关键件

ratLeRep_lit · le_refl · natToRat_mul_rep。

查看解答 定理 · 10 行

具体读数：半的三倍是 3/2，超过 1；两倍恰好是 1，所以弱序也成立。

theorem demo_arch_two :
    ratLeRep ratOneRep
      (ratMulRep (natToRat (Nat.succ (Nat.succ Nat.zero)))
        (qrep 1 Nat.zero (Nat.succ Nat.zero))) :=
  Iff.mpr
    (ratLeRep_lit 1 Nat.zero Nat.zero
      (myMul (Nat.succ (Nat.succ Nat.zero)) 1)
      (myMul (Nat.succ (Nat.succ Nat.zero)) Nat.zero)
      (Nat.succ Nat.zero))
    (le_refl (myMul (Nat.succ (Nat.succ Nat.zero)) 1))

阿基米德性质：反复相加总能超过

7 道练习 · 7 份按名匹配的解答 · 14 个解答独有声明

练习 1 · ratLeRep_zero_lit 定理

0 ≤ x 在字面代表元上就是「分子第二格不超过第一格」。

theorem ratLeRep_zero_lit (a b m : Nat) :
    ratLeRep ratZeroRep (qrep a b m) ↔ LeNat b a :=
  Iff.intro
    (fun (h : ratLeRep ratZeroRep (qrep a b m)) =>
      @Eq.subst.{1} Nat
        (fun (z : Nat) => LeNat b z)
        (Nat.add a Nat.zero) a (add_zero a)
        (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat
          (fun (z : Nat) => LeNat z (Nat.add a Nat.zero))
          (Nat.add Nat.zero b) b (zero_add b)
          (@Eq.subst.{1} (Prod Nat Nat)
            (fun (p : Prod Nat Nat) => leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero Nat.zero) p)
            (leNum (qrep a b m) ratZeroRep) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b)
            (leNum_lit_zero a b m)
            (@Eq.subst.{1} (Prod Nat Nat)
              (fun (p : Prod Nat Nat) =>
                leRep p (leNum (qrep a b m) ratZeroRep))
              (leNum ratZeroRep (qrep a b m))
              (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero Nat.zero)
              (leNum_zero_lit a b m) h))))
    (fun (h : LeNat b a) =>
      @Eq.subst.{1} (Prod Nat Nat)
        (fun (p : Prod Nat Nat) =>
          leRep p (leNum (qrep a b m) ratZeroRep))
        (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero Nat.zero)
        (leNum ratZeroRep (qrep a b m))
        (@Eq.symm.{1} (Prod Nat Nat)
          (leNum ratZeroRep (qrep a b m))
          (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero Nat.zero)
          (leNum_zero_lit a b m))
        (@Eq.subst.{1} (Prod Nat Nat)
          (fun (p : Prod Nat Nat) => leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero Nat.zero) p)
          (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (leNum (qrep a b m) ratZeroRep)
          (@Eq.symm.{1} (Prod Nat Nat)
            (leNum (qrep a b m) ratZeroRep) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b)
            (leNum_lit_zero a b m))
          (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat
            (fun (z : Nat) => LeNat z (Nat.add a Nat.zero))
            b (Nat.add Nat.zero b)
            (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (Nat.add Nat.zero b) b (zero_add b))
            (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat
              (fun (z : Nat) => LeNat b z)
              a (Nat.add a Nat.zero)
              (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (Nat.add a Nat.zero) a (add_zero a)) h))))
练习 2 · ratRel_zero_of_num_eq 定理

分子两格相等时这个有理数就是零。

theorem ratRel_zero_of_num_eq (a b m : Nat) (h : b = a) :
    ratRel ratZeroRep (qrep a b m) :=
  ratRel_symm (qrep a b m) ratZeroRep
    (Iff.mpr (ratRel_zero_iff_num_zero a b m)
      (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
        (Nat.add a Nat.zero)
        a
        (Nat.add Nat.zero b)
        (add_zero a)
        (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
          a b (Nat.add Nat.zero b)
          (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat b a h)
          (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (Nat.add Nat.zero b) b (zero_add b)))))
练习 3 · ratLtRep_zero_num 定理

0 < qrep p q m 说分子第一格严格大于第二格。

theorem ratLtRep_zero_num (a b m : Nat)
    (h : ratLtRep ratZeroRep (qrep a b m)) : LeNat (Nat.succ b) a :=
  Or.elim
    (fun (hba : b = a) =>
      @False.elim (LeNat (Nat.succ b) a)
        ((And.right h) (ratRel_zero_of_num_eq a b m hba)))
    (fun (hlt : LeNat (Nat.succ b) a) => hlt)
    (leNat_eq_or_succ b a
      (Iff.mp (ratLeRep_zero_lit a b m) (And.left h)))
练习 4 · arch_le_lit 定理

字面形式：0 < (p - q)/(m+1) 且 0 ≤ (r - s)/(j+1) 时，取 n = (r+1)(m+1) 就有 (r-s)/(j+1) ≤ n·(p-q)/(m+1)。

theorem arch_le_lit (p q m : Nat) (hpq : ratLtRep ratZeroRep (qrep p q m))
    (r s j : Nat) (hc : ratLeRep ratZeroRep (qrep r s j)) :
    ∃ (n : Nat), ratLeRep (qrep r s j)
      (ratMulRep (natToRat n) (qrep p q m)) :=
  Exists.elim
    (leNat_exists_add (Nat.succ q) p (ratLtRep_zero_num p q m hpq))
    (fun (k : Nat) (hk : p = Nat.add (Nat.succ q) k) =>
      Exists.elim
        (leNat_exists_add s r (Iff.mp (ratLeRep_zero_lit r s j) hc))
        (fun (u : Nat) (hu : r = Nat.add s u) =>
          Exists.intro (myMul (Nat.succ r) (Nat.succ m))
            (@Eq.subst.{1} ratRep
              (fun (z : ratRep) => ratLeRep (qrep r s j) z)
              (qrep
                (myMul (myMul (Nat.succ r) (Nat.succ m)) p)
                (myMul (myMul (Nat.succ r) (Nat.succ m)) q)
                m)
              (ratMulRep (natToRat (myMul (Nat.succ r) (Nat.succ m)))
                (qrep p q m))
              (@Eq.symm.{1} ratRep
                (ratMulRep (natToRat (myMul (Nat.succ r) (Nat.succ m)))
                  (qrep p q m))
                (qrep
                  (myMul (myMul (Nat.succ r) (Nat.succ m)) p)
                  (myMul (myMul (Nat.succ r) (Nat.succ m)) q)
                  m)
                (natToRat_mul_rep (myMul (Nat.succ r) (Nat.succ m)) p q m))
              (Iff.mpr
                (ratLeRep_lit r s j
                  (myMul (myMul (Nat.succ r) (Nat.succ m)) p)
                  (myMul (myMul (Nat.succ r) (Nat.succ m)) q)
                  m)
                (arch_G p q m r s u k j hk hu)))))
练习 5 · arch_le 定理

任意代表元：两条前提换成「正」与「非负」，直接交给字面形式。

theorem arch_le (x c : Prod (Prod Nat Nat) Nat) :
    ratLtRep ratZeroRep x → ratLeRep ratZeroRep c →
      ∃ (n : Nat), ratLeRep c (ratMulRep (natToRat n) x) :=
  match x with
  | prod_mk (prod_mk p q) m =>
    match c with
    | prod_mk (prod_mk r s) j =>
      fun (hx : ratLtRep ratZeroRep (qrep p q m))
          (hc : ratLeRep ratZeroRep (qrep r s j)) =>
        arch_le_lit p q m hx r s j hc
练习 6 · arch_lt_of_nonneg 定理

严格版本：把 c 换成 c + 1 再取同一个倍数。

theorem arch_lt_of_nonneg (x c : ratRep) (hx : ratLtRep ratZeroRep x)
    (hc : ratLeRep ratZeroRep c) :
    ∃ (n : Nat), ratLtRep c (ratMulRep (natToRat n) x) :=
  Exists.elim
    (arch_le x (ratAddRep c ratOneRep) hx
      (ratLeRep_zero_add_nonneg c ratOneRep hc
        (ratLtRep_le ratZeroRep ratOneRep ratLtRep_zero_one)))
    (fun (n : Nat)
        (hn : ratLeRep (ratAddRep c ratOneRep) (ratMulRep (natToRat n) x)) =>
      Exists.intro n
        (ratLtRep_of_lt_le c (ratAddRep c ratOneRep)
          (ratMulRep (natToRat n) x)
          (ratLtRep_self_add_pos c ratOneRep ratLtRep_zero_one)
          hn))
练习 7 · demo_arch_two 定理

theorem demo_arch_two :
    ratLeRep ratOneRep
      (ratMulRep (natToRat (Nat.succ (Nat.succ Nat.zero)))

      (Nat.succ Nat.zero))

补充 · myMul_one_left 定理
theorem myMul_one_left (n : Nat) :
    myMul (Nat.succ Nat.zero) n = n :=
  @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
    (myMul (Nat.succ Nat.zero) n)
    (Nat.add (myMul Nat.zero n) n)
    n
    (succ_mul Nat.zero n)
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (Nat.add (myMul Nat.zero n) n)
      (Nat.add Nat.zero n)
      n
      (@congrArg.{1} Nat Nat (myMul Nat.zero n) Nat.zero
        (fun (z : Nat) => Nat.add z n) (zero_mul n))
      (zero_add n))
补充 · myMul_succ_le 定理
theorem myMul_succ_le (r E : Nat) :
    LeNat (myMul r E) (myMul (Nat.succ r) E) :=
  @Eq.subst.{1} Nat
    (fun (z : Nat) => LeNat (myMul r E) z)
    (Nat.add (myMul r E) E)
    (myMul (Nat.succ r) E)
    (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
      (myMul (Nat.succ r) E) (Nat.add (myMul r E) E) (succ_mul r E))
    (leNat_self_add (myMul r E) E)

theorem leNat_one_mul (t D : Nat)
    (ht : LeNat (Nat.succ Nat.zero) t)
    (hD : LeNat (Nat.succ Nat.zero) D) :
    LeNat (Nat.succ Nat.zero) (myMul t D) :=
  leNat_trans (Nat.succ Nat.zero) D (myMul t D)
    hD
    (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat
      (fun (z : Nat) => LeNat z (myMul t D))
      (myMul (Nat.succ Nat.zero) D) D
      (myMul_one_left D)
      (leNat_mul_right (Nat.succ Nat.zero) t D ht))

theorem leNat_self_mul (n x : Nat)
    (hx : LeNat (Nat.succ Nat.zero) x) :
    LeNat n (myMul n x) :=
  @Eq.subst.{1} Nat
    (fun (z : Nat) => LeNat n z)
    (myMul x n) (myMul n x)
    (myMul_comm x n)

      (fun (z : Nat) => LeNat z (myMul x n))
      (myMul (Nat.succ Nat.zero) n) n
      (myMul_one_left n)
      (leNat_mul_right (Nat.succ Nat.zero) x n hx))

theorem arch_uE_le (r s u m : Nat) (hu : r = Nat.add s u) :
    LeNat (myMul u (Nat.succ m))
      (myMul (Nat.succ r) (Nat.succ m)) :=

      (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat
        (fun (z : Nat) => LeNat u z)
        (Nat.add s u) r
        (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat r (Nat.add s u) hu)
        (leNat_left_of_add s u)))
    (myMul_succ_le r (Nat.succ m))

theorem arch_n_le (r m k j : Nat) :
    LeNat (myMul (Nat.succ r) (Nat.succ m))
      (myMul (myMul (myMul (Nat.succ r) (Nat.succ m)) (Nat.succ k))
        (Nat.succ j)) :=
  @Eq.subst.{1} Nat
    (fun (z : Nat) => LeNat (myMul (Nat.succ r) (Nat.succ m)) z)
    (myMul (myMul (Nat.succ r) (Nat.succ m))
      (myMul (Nat.succ k) (Nat.succ j)))
    (myMul (myMul (myMul (Nat.succ r) (Nat.succ m)) (Nat.succ k))
      (Nat.succ j))
    (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
      (myMul (myMul (myMul (Nat.succ r) (Nat.succ m)) (Nat.succ k))
        (Nat.succ j))
      (myMul (myMul (Nat.succ r) (Nat.succ m))
        (myMul (Nat.succ k) (Nat.succ j)))
      (myMul_assoc (myMul (Nat.succ r) (Nat.succ m))
        (Nat.succ k) (Nat.succ j)))
    (leNat_self_mul (myMul (Nat.succ r) (Nat.succ m))
      (myMul (Nat.succ k) (Nat.succ j))
      (leNat_one_mul (Nat.succ k) (Nat.succ j)
        (leNat_succ_succ Nat.zero k (leNat_zero_le k))
        (leNat_succ_succ Nat.zero j (leNat_zero_le j))))

theorem arch_LHS_eq (r s u m n q j : Nat) (hu : r = Nat.add s u) :
    Nat.add
      (Nat.add (myMul u (Nat.succ m)) (myMul (myMul n q) (Nat.succ j)))

      (Nat.add (myMul u (Nat.succ m)) (myMul (myMul n q) (Nat.succ j)))
      (myMul s (Nat.succ m)))

补充 · arch_RHS_eq 定理
theorem arch_RHS_eq (p q m n s k j : Nat)
    (hnp : myMul n p =
      Nat.add (myMul n q) (myMul n (Nat.succ k))) :
    Nat.add
      (Nat.add (myMul (myMul n (Nat.succ k)) (Nat.succ j))
        (myMul (myMul n q) (Nat.succ j)))
      (myMul s (Nat.succ m))
    = Nat.add (myMul (myMul n p) (Nat.succ j)) (myMul s (Nat.succ m)) :=
  @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
    (Nat.add
      (Nat.add (myMul (myMul n (Nat.succ k)) (Nat.succ j))
        (myMul (myMul n q) (Nat.succ j)))
      (myMul s (Nat.succ m)))
    (Nat.add
      (Nat.add (myMul (myMul n q) (Nat.succ j))
        (myMul (myMul n (Nat.succ k)) (Nat.succ j)))
      (myMul s (Nat.succ m)))
    (Nat.add (myMul (myMul n p) (Nat.succ j)) (myMul s (Nat.succ m)))
    (@congrArg.{1} Nat Nat
      (Nat.add (myMul (myMul n (Nat.succ k)) (Nat.succ j))
        (myMul (myMul n q) (Nat.succ j)))
      (Nat.add (myMul (myMul n q) (Nat.succ j))
        (myMul (myMul n (Nat.succ k)) (Nat.succ j)))
      (fun (z : Nat) => Nat.add z (myMul s (Nat.succ m)))
      (add_comm (myMul (myMul n (Nat.succ k)) (Nat.succ j))
        (myMul (myMul n q) (Nat.succ j))))
    (@congrArg.{1} Nat Nat
      (Nat.add (myMul (myMul n q) (Nat.succ j))
        (myMul (myMul n (Nat.succ k)) (Nat.succ j)))
      (myMul (myMul n p) (Nat.succ j))
      (fun (z : Nat) => Nat.add z (myMul s (Nat.succ m)))
      (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
        (myMul (myMul n p) (Nat.succ j))
        (Nat.add (myMul (myMul n q) (Nat.succ j))
          (myMul (myMul n (Nat.succ k)) (Nat.succ j)))
        (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
          (myMul (myMul n p) (Nat.succ j))
          (myMul (Nat.add (myMul n q) (myMul n (Nat.succ k))) (Nat.succ j))
          (Nat.add (myMul (myMul n q) (Nat.succ j))
            (myMul (myMul n (Nat.succ k)) (Nat.succ j)))
          (@congrArg.{1} Nat Nat
            (myMul n p) (Nat.add (myMul n q) (myMul n (Nat.succ k)))
            (fun (z : Nat) => myMul z (Nat.succ j)) hnp)
          (mul_add_right (myMul n q) (myMul n (Nat.succ k)) (Nat.succ j)))))
补充 · arch_h10 定理

    (hk : p = Nat.add (Nat.succ q) k)

III.8 · 本章第 14 / 16 单元 · 全书第 164 / 171 单元

164细分割上的振幅：一致连续把曲线压成窄带

Unit 164 — Oscillation on a Fine Partition: Uniform Continuity Traps the Curve in a Strip

3 个定义 · 8 个定理 · 8 道练习 · 24 条提示 · 解答附录

↑ 目录

一致连续说的是：只要两点足够靠近，它们的函数值就足够靠近。要把这句话用在面积上，还得把它落到分割的每一段里。一段的左端点是这一段的起点，段里的任何一点到它的距离都不超过段长；如果每一段都短于 δ，那么整段上的函数值与左端点处的值之差就都小于 η。

于是每一段的上方可以架一条常数上界 f(左端点) + η，下方可以垫一条常数下界 f(左端点) - η。曲线被夹在两条台阶之间，两条台阶的差只由 η 与区间长度决定：每一段的高度差都是 η + η，乘上段宽再逐段相加，就是 (η + η) 乘总长。

这条估计是可积性证明的核心：只要能把分割造得足够细，上下和之差就能压到任意小。造分割要用阿基米德性质把区间等分，这一步留给下一单元。

分割固定不动，函数取 ratRep → ratRep，停在有理代表元层。

每段都短于 delta

「分割够细」的可判读形式：每一段的宽都小于 delta。

定义MeshBelow声明 1
def MeshBelow (p : Partition) (delta : ratRep) : Prop :=
  ∀ (i : Nat), LeNat (Nat.succ i) (partCount p) → ratLtRep (segLen p i) delta

左端点处的下界：函数值减去 eta。

定义leftLower声明 2
def leftLower (f : ratRep → ratRep) (p : Partition) (eta : ratRep)
    (i : Nat) : ratRep :=
  ratAddRep (f (partPoint p i)) (ratNegRep eta)

左端点处的上界：函数值加上 eta。

定义leftUpper声明 3
def leftUpper (f : ratRep → ratRep) (p : Partition) (eta : ratRep)
    (i : Nat) : ratRep :=
  ratAddRep (f (partPoint p i)) eta
练习 1segLen_add_point定理附录解答

一段的右端是左端加段宽

theorem segLen_add_point (p : Partition) (i : Nat) :
    ratRel (ratAddRep (partPoint p i) (segLen p i))
      (partPoint p (Nat.succ i)) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

把段宽展开成「右端减左端」；(x - y) + y ~ x 把减法收回，注意加法要交换到「差在左」的形状。

提示 2目标形态

ratRel (partPoint p i + segLen p i) (partPoint p (succ i))。

提示 3关键件

ratAdd_comm · ratAddRep_wd_gen · ratSubRep_add_self。

theorem segLen_add_point (p : Partition) (i : Nat) :
    ratRel (ratAddRep (partPoint p i) (segLen p i))
      (partPoint p (Nat.succ i)) :=
  ratRel_trans_gen
    (ratAddRep (partPoint p i) (segLen p i))
    (ratAddRep (segLen p i) (partPoint p i))
    (partPoint p (Nat.succ i))
    (ratAdd_comm (partPoint p i) (segLen p i))
    (ratSubRep_add_self (partPoint p (Nat.succ i)) (partPoint p i))
练习 2close_point_left定理附录解答

段里的点靠近左端点

theorem close_point_left (a b : ratRep) (p : Partition) (delta : ratRep)
    (hm : MeshBelow p delta) (i : Nat)
    (hi : LeNat (Nat.succ i) (partCount p)) (x : ratRep)
    (hx : InClosed (partPoint p i) (partPoint p (Nat.succ i)) x) :
    close x (partPoint p i) delta :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

段里的点不小于左端、不大于右端，而右端等于左端加段宽；两侧一夹得到「左端 ≤ x ≤ 左端 + 段宽」，段宽又小于 delta，于是 close x 左端 delta。

提示 2目标形态

close x (partPoint p i) delta。

提示 3关键件

inClosed_left · inClosed_right · segLen_add_point · ratLeRep_congr_gen · close_of_le_ub。

查看解答 定理 · 14 行
theorem close_point_left (a b : ratRep) (p : Partition) (delta : ratRep)
    (hm : MeshBelow p delta) (i : Nat)
    (hi : LeNat (Nat.succ i) (partCount p)) (x : ratRep)
    (hx : InClosed (partPoint p i) (partPoint p (Nat.succ i)) x) :
    close x (partPoint p i) delta :=
  close_of_le_ub x (partPoint p i) delta (segLen p i)
    (inClosed_left (partPoint p i) (partPoint p (Nat.succ i)) x hx)
    (ratLeRep_congr_gen x x (partPoint p (Nat.succ i))
      (ratAddRep (partPoint p i) (segLen p i))
      (ratRel_refl x)
      (ratRel_symm (ratAddRep (partPoint p i) (segLen p i))
        (partPoint p (Nat.succ i)) (segLen_add_point p i))
      (inClosed_right (partPoint p i) (partPoint p (Nat.succ i)) x hx))
    (hm i hi)

由段里的点靠近左端点与一致连续的 delta-eta 条件，得到函数值靠近左端点处的值；close_lt_add_right 把接近性换成严格上界，再投成弱序。

theorem upperStep_leftUpper (a b : ratRep) (p : Partition)
    (f : ratRep → ratRep) (eta delta : ratRep) (hm : MeshBelow p delta)
    (hu : ∀ (x y : ratRep), close x y delta → close (f x) (f y) eta) :
    UpperStep a b p f (leftUpper f p eta) :=
  fun (i : Nat) (hi : LeNat (Nat.succ i) (partCount p)) (x : ratRep)
      (hx : InClosed (partPoint p i) (partPoint p (Nat.succ i)) x) =>
    ratLtRep_le (f x) (ratAddRep (f (partPoint p i)) eta)
      (close_lt_add_right (f x) (f (partPoint p i)) eta
        (hu x (partPoint p i)
          (close_point_left a b p delta hm i hi x hx)))

theorem lowerStep_leftLower (a b : ratRep) (p : Partition)
    (f : ratRep → ratRep) (eta delta : ratRep) (hm : MeshBelow p delta)
    (hu : ∀ (x y : ratRep), close x y delta → close (f x) (f y) eta) :

  sorry
提示 1思路

同样的接近性把左端点处的值压在函数值上方 eta 以内；close_neg_bound 给出「左端点的值减函数值 ≤ eta」，再用「L - x ≤ eta ⇒ L - eta ≤ x」把它翻成下界。
