theorem lowerStep_leftLower (a b : ratRep) (p : Partition)
    (f : ratRep → ratRep) (eta delta : ratRep) (hm : MeshBelow p delta)
    (hu : ∀ (x y : ratRep), close x y delta → close (f x) (f y) eta) :
    LowerStep a b p f (leftLower f p eta) :=
  fun (i : Nat) (hi : LeNat (Nat.succ i) (partCount p)) (x : ratRep)
      (hx : InClosed (partPoint p i) (partPoint p (Nat.succ i)) x) =>
    le_sub_of_sub_le (f (partPoint p i)) (f x) eta
      (close_neg_bound (f x) (f (partPoint p i)) eta
        (hu x (partPoint p i)
          (close_point_left a b p delta hm i hi x hx)))
练习 5le_sub_of_sub_le定理附录解答

一个顺序小工具

theorem le_sub_of_sub_le (L x eta : ratRep)
    (h : ratLeRep (ratSubRep L x) eta) :
    ratLeRep (ratSubRep L eta) x :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

从 L - x ≤ eta 出发，两边加 x 得到 L ≤ x + eta；再在右边减去 eta。右端 (x + eta) - eta 先交换中间项，结合后 eta + (-eta) 收成零，再加零。

提示 2目标形态

ratLeRep (L - eta) x。

提示 3关键件

le_add_of_sub_le · ratLeRep_add_right · ratAdd_comm · ratAdd_assoc · ratNegRep_add_self · ratAdd_zero。

查看解答 定理 · 21 行

两条台阶

theorem le_sub_of_sub_le (L x eta : ratRep)
    (h : ratLeRep (ratSubRep L x) eta) :
    ratLeRep (ratSubRep L eta) x :=
  ratLeRep_congr_gen
    (ratSubRep L eta) (ratSubRep L eta)
    (ratAddRep (ratAddRep x eta) (ratNegRep eta)) x
    (ratRel_refl (ratSubRep L eta))
    (ratRel_trans_gen
      (ratAddRep (ratAddRep x eta) (ratNegRep eta))
      (ratAddRep x (ratAddRep eta (ratNegRep eta)))
      x
      (ratAdd_assoc x eta (ratNegRep eta))
      (ratRel_trans_gen
        (ratAddRep x (ratAddRep eta (ratNegRep eta)))
        (ratAddRep x ratZeroRep)
        x
        (ratAddRep_wd_snd_gen x (ratAddRep eta (ratNegRep eta))
          ratZeroRep (ratAdd_neg_gen eta))
        (ratAdd_zero x)))
    (ratLeRep_add_right L (ratAddRep x eta) (ratNegRep eta)
      (le_add_of_sub_le L x eta h))
练习 6stepSum_left_gap定理附录解答

两条台阶的差

theorem stepSum_left_gap (a b : ratRep) (p : Partition) (f : ratRep → ratRep)
    (hp : IsPartition a b p) (eta : ratRep) :
    ratRel
      (ratSubRep (stepSum p (leftUpper f p eta))
        (stepSum p (leftLower f p eta)))
      (ratAddRep (ratMulRep eta (ratSubRep b a))
        (ratMulRep eta (ratSubRep b a))) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

把上和分成「左端点值之和」加「常值 eta 之和」，下和分成「左端点值之和」加「常值 -eta 之和」；两个常值面积都化成 eta 乘 (b - a)，差的公共部分消掉，剩下 eta(b-a) 减去 -eta(b-a)，即两段相加。

提示 2目标形态

ratRel (stepSum p (leftUpper f p eta) - stepSum p (leftLower f p eta)) (eta(b-a) + eta(b-a))。

提示 3关键件

stepSum_add · stepSum_const · ratSubRep_add_cancel · ratAdd_comm · ratMul_neg · ratNegRep_invol · ratAddRep_wd_snd_gen。

查看解答 定理 · 135 行

一个顺序小工具

theorem stepSum_left_gap (a b : ratRep) (p : Partition) (f : ratRep → ratRep)
    (hp : IsPartition a b p) (eta : ratRep) :
    ratRel
      (ratSubRep (stepSum p (leftUpper f p eta))
        (stepSum p (leftLower f p eta)))
      (ratAddRep (ratMulRep eta (ratSubRep b a))
        (ratMulRep eta (ratSubRep b a))) :=
  ratRel_trans_gen
    (ratSubRep (stepSum p (leftUpper f p eta))
      (stepSum p (leftLower f p eta)))
    (ratSubRep
      (ratAddRep (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i)))
        (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta)))
      (ratAddRep (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i)))
        (ratNegRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta)))))
    (ratAddRep (ratMulRep eta (ratSubRep b a))
      (ratMulRep eta (ratSubRep b a)))
    (ratSubRep_wd
      (stepSum p (leftUpper f p eta))
      (ratAddRep (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i)))
        (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta)))
      (stepSum p (leftLower f p eta))
      (ratAddRep (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i)))
        (ratNegRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))))
      (stepSum_add p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i)) (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))
      (ratRel_trans_gen
        (stepSum p (leftLower f p eta))
        (ratAddRep (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i)))
          (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => ratNegRep eta)))
        (ratAddRep (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i)))
          (ratNegRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))))
        (stepSum_add p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i))
          (fun (_ : Nat) => ratNegRep eta))
        (ratAddRep_wd_snd_gen
          (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i)))
          (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => ratNegRep eta))
          (ratNegRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta)))
          (ratRel_trans_gen
            (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => ratNegRep eta))
            (ratMulRep (ratNegRep eta) (ratSubRep b a))
            (ratNegRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta)))
            (stepSum_const a b p hp (ratNegRep eta))
            (ratRel_trans_gen
              (ratMulRep (ratNegRep eta) (ratSubRep b a))
              (ratNegRep (ratMulRep (ratSubRep b a) eta))
              (ratNegRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta)))
              (ratRel_trans_gen
                (ratMulRep (ratNegRep eta) (ratSubRep b a))
                (ratMulRep (ratSubRep b a) (ratNegRep eta))
                (ratNegRep (ratMulRep (ratSubRep b a) eta))
                (ratMul_comm (ratNegRep eta) (ratSubRep b a))
                (ratMul_neg (ratSubRep b a) eta))
              (ratNegRep_wd (ratMulRep (ratSubRep b a) eta)
                (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))
                (ratRel_symm (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))
                  (ratMulRep (ratSubRep b a) eta)
                  (ratRel_trans_gen
                    (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))
                    (ratMulRep eta (ratSubRep b a))
                    (ratMulRep (ratSubRep b a) eta)
                    (stepSum_const a b p hp eta)
                    (ratMul_comm eta (ratSubRep b a))))))))))
    (ratRel_trans_gen
      (ratSubRep
        (ratAddRep (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i)))
          (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta)))
        (ratAddRep (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i)))
          (ratNegRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta)))))
      (ratSubRep
        (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))
        (ratNegRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))))
      (ratAddRep (ratMulRep eta (ratSubRep b a))
        (ratMulRep eta (ratSubRep b a)))
      (ratRel_trans_gen
        (ratSubRep
          (ratAddRep (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i)))
            (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta)))
          (ratAddRep (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i)))
            (ratNegRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta)))))
        (ratSubRep
          (ratAddRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))
            (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i))))
          (ratAddRep
            (ratNegRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta)))
            (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i)))))
        (ratSubRep
          (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))
          (ratNegRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))))
        (ratSubRep_wd
          (ratAddRep (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i)))
            (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta)))
          (ratAddRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))
            (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i))))
          (ratAddRep (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i)))
            (ratNegRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))))
          (ratAddRep
            (ratNegRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta)))
            (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i))))
          (ratAdd_comm (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i)))
            (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta)))
          (ratAdd_comm (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i)))
            (ratNegRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta)))))
        (ratSubRep_add_cancel
          (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))
          (ratNegRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta)))
          (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i)))))
      (ratRel_trans_gen
        (ratSubRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))
          (ratNegRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))))
        (ratAddRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))
          (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta)))
        (ratAddRep (ratMulRep eta (ratSubRep b a))
          (ratMulRep eta (ratSubRep b a)))
        (ratAddRep_wd_snd_gen
          (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))
          (ratNegRep (ratNegRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))))
          (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))
          (ratNegRep_invol (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))))
        (ratRel_trans_gen
          (ratAddRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))
            (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta)))
          (ratAddRep (ratMulRep eta (ratSubRep b a))
            (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta)))
          (ratAddRep (ratMulRep eta (ratSubRep b a))
            (ratMulRep eta (ratSubRep b a)))
          (ratAddRep_wd_gen
            (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))
            (ratMulRep eta (ratSubRep b a))
            (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))
            (stepSum_const a b p hp eta))
          (ratAddRep_wd_snd_gen
            (ratMulRep eta (ratSubRep b a))
            (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))
            (ratMulRep eta (ratSubRep b a))
            (stepSum_const a b p hp eta)))))
练习 7strip_of_mesh定理附录解答

夹住曲线的一对台阶

theorem strip_of_mesh (a b : ratRep) (p : Partition) (f : ratRep → ratRep)
    (hp : IsPartition a b p) (eta delta : ratRep) (hm : MeshBelow p delta)
    (hu : ∀ (x y : ratRep), close x y delta → close (f x) (f y) eta) :
    ∃ (l : Nat → ratRep), ∃ (u : Nat → ratRep),
      LowerStep a b p f l ∧ UpperStep a b p f u ∧
        ratRel (ratSubRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l))
          (ratAddRep (ratMulRep eta (ratSubRep b a))
            (ratMulRep eta (ratSubRep b a))) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

把上面三条合起来：下界、上界都由同一段估计给出，两个矩形和之差由差公式收成两段 eta 乘总长。

提示 2目标形态

存在两条台阶 l、u，它们分别是下界与上界，且 stepSum u - stepSum l ~ eta(b-a) + eta(b-a)。

提示 3关键件

lowerStep_leftLower · upperStep_leftUpper · stepSum_left_gap · Exists.intro · And.intro。

查看解答 定理 · 15 行
theorem strip_of_mesh (a b : ratRep) (p : Partition) (f : ratRep → ratRep)
    (hp : IsPartition a b p) (eta delta : ratRep) (hm : MeshBelow p delta)
    (hu : ∀ (x y : ratRep), close x y delta → close (f x) (f y) eta) :
    ∃ (l : Nat → ratRep), ∃ (u : Nat → ratRep),
      LowerStep a b p f l ∧ UpperStep a b p f u ∧
        ratRel (ratSubRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l))
          (ratAddRep (ratMulRep eta (ratSubRep b a))
            (ratMulRep eta (ratSubRep b a))) :=
  Exists.intro (leftLower f p eta)
    (Exists.intro (leftUpper f p eta)
      (And.intro
        (lowerStep_leftLower a b p f eta delta hm hu)
        (And.intro
          (upperStep_leftUpper a b p f eta delta hm hu)
          (stepSum_left_gap a b p f hp eta))))
练习 8demo_twoPoint_half定理附录解答

可算的读数：常数曲线在两点分割上

theorem demo_twoPoint_half :
    ratRel
      (ratSubRep
        (stepSum (twoPoint ratZeroRep ratOneRep)
          (leftUpper (fun (_ : ratRep) => ratZeroRep)
            (twoPoint ratZeroRep ratOneRep) (ratHalf ratOneRep)))
        (stepSum (twoPoint ratZeroRep ratOneRep)
          (leftLower (fun (_ : ratRep) => ratZeroRep)
            (twoPoint ratZeroRep ratOneRep) (ratHalf ratOneRep))))
      ratOneRep :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

常数函数没有起伏，两条台阶的高就是正负 eta；两点分割只有一段，宽是 b - a。矩形和之差是 eta(b-a) 加 eta(b-a)，而 eta 取一半时两半相加正好是 1。

提示 2目标形态

ratRel (stepSum (twoPoint 0 1) 上半 - stepSum (twoPoint 0 1) 下半) 1。

提示 3关键件

stepSum_left_gap · twoPoint_isPartition · ratLtRep_zero_one · ratMul_one_right · ratHalf_add_self · ratSubRep_self。

细分割上的振幅：一致连续把曲线压成窄带

练习 1 · segLen_add_point 定理
theorem segLen_add_point (p : Partition) (i : Nat) :
    ratRel (ratAddRep (partPoint p i) (segLen p i))
      (partPoint p (Nat.succ i)) :=
  ratRel_trans_gen
    (ratAddRep (partPoint p i) (segLen p i))
    (ratAddRep (segLen p i) (partPoint p i))
    (partPoint p (Nat.succ i))
    (ratAdd_comm (partPoint p i) (segLen p i))
    (ratSubRep_add_self (partPoint p (Nat.succ i)) (partPoint p i))
练习 2 · close_point_left 定理
theorem close_point_left (a b : ratRep) (p : Partition) (delta : ratRep)
    (hm : MeshBelow p delta) (i : Nat)
    (hi : LeNat (Nat.succ i) (partCount p)) (x : ratRep)
    (hx : InClosed (partPoint p i) (partPoint p (Nat.succ i)) x) :
    close x (partPoint p i) delta :=
  close_of_le_ub x (partPoint p i) delta (segLen p i)
    (inClosed_left (partPoint p i) (partPoint p (Nat.succ i)) x hx)
    (ratLeRep_congr_gen x x (partPoint p (Nat.succ i))
      (ratAddRep (partPoint p i) (segLen p i))
      (ratRel_refl x)
      (ratRel_symm (ratAddRep (partPoint p i) (segLen p i))
        (partPoint p (Nat.succ i)) (segLen_add_point p i))
      (inClosed_right (partPoint p i) (partPoint p (Nat.succ i)) x hx))
    (hm i hi)
练习 3 · upperStep_leftUpper 定理
theorem upperStep_leftUpper (a b : ratRep) (p : Partition)
    (f : ratRep → ratRep) (eta delta : ratRep) (hm : MeshBelow p delta)
    (hu : ∀ (x y : ratRep), close x y delta → close (f x) (f y) eta) :
    UpperStep a b p f (leftUpper f p eta) :=
  fun (i : Nat) (hi : LeNat (Nat.succ i) (partCount p)) (x : ratRep)
      (hx : InClosed (partPoint p i) (partPoint p (Nat.succ i)) x) =>
    ratLtRep_le (f x) (ratAddRep (f (partPoint p i)) eta)
      (close_lt_add_right (f x) (f (partPoint p i)) eta
        (hu x (partPoint p i)
          (close_point_left a b p delta hm i hi x hx)))
练习 4 · lowerStep_leftLower 定理
theorem lowerStep_leftLower (a b : ratRep) (p : Partition)
    (f : ratRep → ratRep) (eta delta : ratRep) (hm : MeshBelow p delta)
    (hu : ∀ (x y : ratRep), close x y delta → close (f x) (f y) eta) :
    LowerStep a b p f (leftLower f p eta) :=
  fun (i : Nat) (hi : LeNat (Nat.succ i) (partCount p)) (x : ratRep)
      (hx : InClosed (partPoint p i) (partPoint p (Nat.succ i)) x) =>
    le_sub_of_sub_le (f (partPoint p i)) (f x) eta
      (close_neg_bound (f x) (f (partPoint p i)) eta
        (hu x (partPoint p i)
          (close_point_left a b p delta hm i hi x hx)))
练习 5 · le_sub_of_sub_le 定理

两条台阶

theorem le_sub_of_sub_le (L x eta : ratRep)
    (h : ratLeRep (ratSubRep L x) eta) :
    ratLeRep (ratSubRep L eta) x :=
  ratLeRep_congr_gen
    (ratSubRep L eta) (ratSubRep L eta)
    (ratAddRep (ratAddRep x eta) (ratNegRep eta)) x
    (ratRel_refl (ratSubRep L eta))
    (ratRel_trans_gen
      (ratAddRep (ratAddRep x eta) (ratNegRep eta))
      (ratAddRep x (ratAddRep eta (ratNegRep eta)))
      x
      (ratAdd_assoc x eta (ratNegRep eta))
      (ratRel_trans_gen
        (ratAddRep x (ratAddRep eta (ratNegRep eta)))
        (ratAddRep x ratZeroRep)
        x
        (ratAddRep_wd_snd_gen x (ratAddRep eta (ratNegRep eta))
          ratZeroRep (ratAdd_neg_gen eta))
        (ratAdd_zero x)))
    (ratLeRep_add_right L (ratAddRep x eta) (ratNegRep eta)
      (le_add_of_sub_le L x eta h))
练习 6 · stepSum_left_gap 定理

一个顺序小工具

theorem stepSum_left_gap (a b : ratRep) (p : Partition) (f : ratRep → ratRep)
    (hp : IsPartition a b p) (eta : ratRep) :
    ratRel
      (ratSubRep (stepSum p (leftUpper f p eta))
        (stepSum p (leftLower f p eta)))
      (ratAddRep (ratMulRep eta (ratSubRep b a))
        (ratMulRep eta (ratSubRep b a))) :=
  ratRel_trans_gen
    (ratSubRep (stepSum p (leftUpper f p eta))
      (stepSum p (leftLower f p eta)))
    (ratSubRep
      (ratAddRep (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i)))
        (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta)))
      (ratAddRep (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i)))
        (ratNegRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta)))))
    (ratAddRep (ratMulRep eta (ratSubRep b a))
      (ratMulRep eta (ratSubRep b a)))
    (ratSubRep_wd
      (stepSum p (leftUpper f p eta))
      (ratAddRep (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i)))
        (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta)))
      (stepSum p (leftLower f p eta))
      (ratAddRep (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i)))
        (ratNegRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))))
      (stepSum_add p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i)) (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))
      (ratRel_trans_gen
        (stepSum p (leftLower f p eta))
        (ratAddRep (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i)))
          (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => ratNegRep eta)))
        (ratAddRep (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i)))
          (ratNegRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))))
        (stepSum_add p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i))
          (fun (_ : Nat) => ratNegRep eta))
        (ratAddRep_wd_snd_gen
          (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i)))
          (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => ratNegRep eta))
          (ratNegRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta)))
          (ratRel_trans_gen
            (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => ratNegRep eta))
            (ratMulRep (ratNegRep eta) (ratSubRep b a))
            (ratNegRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta)))
            (stepSum_const a b p hp (ratNegRep eta))
            (ratRel_trans_gen
              (ratMulRep (ratNegRep eta) (ratSubRep b a))
              (ratNegRep (ratMulRep (ratSubRep b a) eta))
              (ratNegRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta)))
              (ratRel_trans_gen
                (ratMulRep (ratNegRep eta) (ratSubRep b a))
                (ratMulRep (ratSubRep b a) (ratNegRep eta))
                (ratNegRep (ratMulRep (ratSubRep b a) eta))
                (ratMul_comm (ratNegRep eta) (ratSubRep b a))
                (ratMul_neg (ratSubRep b a) eta))
              (ratNegRep_wd (ratMulRep (ratSubRep b a) eta)
                (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))
                (ratRel_symm (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))
                  (ratMulRep (ratSubRep b a) eta)
                  (ratRel_trans_gen
                    (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))
                    (ratMulRep eta (ratSubRep b a))
                    (ratMulRep (ratSubRep b a) eta)
                    (stepSum_const a b p hp eta)
                    (ratMul_comm eta (ratSubRep b a))))))))))
    (ratRel_trans_gen
      (ratSubRep
        (ratAddRep (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i)))
          (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta)))
        (ratAddRep (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i)))
          (ratNegRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta)))))
      (ratSubRep
        (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))
        (ratNegRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))))
      (ratAddRep (ratMulRep eta (ratSubRep b a))
        (ratMulRep eta (ratSubRep b a)))
      (ratRel_trans_gen
        (ratSubRep
          (ratAddRep (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i)))
            (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta)))
          (ratAddRep (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i)))
            (ratNegRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta)))))
        (ratSubRep
          (ratAddRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))
            (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i))))
          (ratAddRep
            (ratNegRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta)))
            (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i)))))
        (ratSubRep
          (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))
          (ratNegRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))))
        (ratSubRep_wd
          (ratAddRep (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i)))
            (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta)))
          (ratAddRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))
            (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i))))
          (ratAddRep (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i)))
            (ratNegRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))))
          (ratAddRep
            (ratNegRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta)))
            (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i))))
          (ratAdd_comm (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i)))
            (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta)))
          (ratAdd_comm (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i)))
            (ratNegRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta)))))
        (ratSubRep_add_cancel
          (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))
          (ratNegRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta)))
          (stepSum p (fun (i : Nat) => f (partPoint p i)))))
      (ratRel_trans_gen
        (ratSubRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))
          (ratNegRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))))
        (ratAddRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))
          (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta)))
        (ratAddRep (ratMulRep eta (ratSubRep b a))
          (ratMulRep eta (ratSubRep b a)))
        (ratAddRep_wd_snd_gen
          (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))
          (ratNegRep (ratNegRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))))
          (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))
          (ratNegRep_invol (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))))
        (ratRel_trans_gen
          (ratAddRep (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))
            (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta)))
          (ratAddRep (ratMulRep eta (ratSubRep b a))
            (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta)))
          (ratAddRep (ratMulRep eta (ratSubRep b a))
            (ratMulRep eta (ratSubRep b a)))
          (ratAddRep_wd_gen
            (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))
            (ratMulRep eta (ratSubRep b a))
            (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))
            (stepSum_const a b p hp eta))
          (ratAddRep_wd_snd_gen
            (ratMulRep eta (ratSubRep b a))
            (stepSum p (fun (_ : Nat) => eta))
            (ratMulRep eta (ratSubRep b a))
            (stepSum_const a b p hp eta)))))
练习 7 · strip_of_mesh 定理
theorem strip_of_mesh (a b : ratRep) (p : Partition) (f : ratRep → ratRep)
    (hp : IsPartition a b p) (eta delta : ratRep) (hm : MeshBelow p delta)
    (hu : ∀ (x y : ratRep), close x y delta → close (f x) (f y) eta) :
    ∃ (l : Nat → ratRep), ∃ (u : Nat → ratRep),
      LowerStep a b p f l ∧ UpperStep a b p f u ∧
        ratRel (ratSubRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l))
          (ratAddRep (ratMulRep eta (ratSubRep b a))
            (ratMulRep eta (ratSubRep b a))) :=
  Exists.intro (leftLower f p eta)
    (Exists.intro (leftUpper f p eta)
      (And.intro
        (lowerStep_leftLower a b p f eta delta hm hu)
        (And.intro
          (upperStep_leftUpper a b p f eta delta hm hu)
          (stepSum_left_gap a b p f hp eta))))

      (stepSum (twoPoint ratZeroRep ratOneRep)
        (leftUpper (fun (_ : ratRep) => ratZeroRep)
          (twoPoint ratZeroRep ratOneRep) (ratHalf ratOneRep)))

        (leftLower (fun (_ : ratRep) => ratZeroRep)
          (twoPoint ratZeroRep ratOneRep) (ratHalf ratOneRep))))
    (ratAddRep (ratMulRep (ratHalf ratOneRep) (ratSubRep ratOneRep ratZeroRep)) (ratMulRep (ratHalf ratOneRep) (ratSubRep ratOneRep ratZeroRep)))
    ratOneRep
    (stepSum_left_gap ratZeroRep ratOneRep
      (twoPoint ratZeroRep ratOneRep)
      (fun (_ : ratRep) => ratZeroRep)
      (twoPoint_isPartition ratZeroRep ratOneRep ratLtRep_zero_one)
      (ratHalf ratOneRep))
    (ratRel_trans_gen
      (ratAddRep (ratMulRep (ratHalf ratOneRep) (ratSubRep ratOneRep ratZeroRep)) (ratMulRep (ratHalf ratOneRep) (ratSubRep ratOneRep ratZeroRep)))
      (ratAddRep (ratHalf ratOneRep) (ratHalf ratOneRep))
      ratOneRep
      (ratRel_trans_gen
        (ratAddRep (ratMulRep (ratHalf ratOneRep) (ratSubRep ratOneRep ratZeroRep)) (ratMulRep (ratHalf ratOneRep) (ratSubRep ratOneRep ratZeroRep)))
        (ratAddRep (ratHalf ratOneRep) (ratMulRep (ratHalf ratOneRep) (ratSubRep ratOneRep ratZeroRep)))
        (ratAddRep (ratHalf ratOneRep) (ratHalf ratOneRep))

      (ratMulRep (ratHalf ratOneRep) (ratSubRep ratOneRep ratZeroRep))
      (ratAddRep (ratMulRep (ratHalf ratOneRep) ratOneRep)
        (ratMulRep (ratHalf ratOneRep) ratZeroRep))
      (ratHalf ratOneRep)
      (ratMul_add (ratHalf ratOneRep) ratOneRep ratZeroRep)
      (ratRel_trans_gen

          (ratMulRep (ratHalf ratOneRep) ratZeroRep))

        (ratAddRep_wd_snd_gen (ratHalf ratOneRep) (ratMulRep (ratHalf ratOneRep) (ratSubRep ratOneRep ratZeroRep))
          (ratHalf ratOneRep) (ratRel_trans_gen
      (ratMulRep (ratHalf ratOneRep) (ratSubRep ratOneRep ratZeroRep))
      (ratAddRep (ratMulRep (ratHalf ratOneRep) ratOneRep)

            ratZeroRep (ratMul_zero_right (ratHalf ratOneRep)))
          (ratAdd_zero (ratHalf ratOneRep)))))))
      (ratHalf_add_self ratOneRep))

III.8 · 本章第 15 / 16 单元 · 全书第 165 / 171 单元

165等分分割：把区间切得足够细

Unit 165 — Uniform Partitions: Cutting the Interval Fine Enough

1 个定义 · 8 个定理 · 8 道练习 · 24 条提示 · 解答附录

↑ 目录

要把上下和之差压到 ε 以下，得先有一个分割，使每一段都短于给定的 δ。区间长度 b - a 是固定的，段数可以自己选。段数取得够多，每段就够短；但「够多」不是一个数字，而是一条依赖 δ 的条件。

把区间等分是最省事的造法。取 N 段，每段的宽是 (b-a)/N。阿基米德性质给出 N，使 N·δ 超过 b-a；除回来，每段的宽就短于 δ。剩下的问题是端点：前 N-1 个点是 a + i·(b-a)/N，最后一个点必须正好是 b，不能只是与 b 相等。构造用一个按步数递减的行走函数：走完 N-1 步就停在 b，于是端点由构造保证，不需要在函数里比较下标。

三段的样本 [0,1] 最能看清这件事：步长 1/3，切点是 0、1/3、2/3、1，每段都短于 1。一般情形由同一条构造给出，δ 取什么都可以。

三段的样本

把 [0,1] 等分成三段：段数是 3，切点是 0、1/3、2/3、1。

def threePartition : Partition :=
  uniformPartition ratZeroRep ratOneRep (Nat.succ (Nat.succ Nat.zero))

theorem meshStep_three_lt_one :
    ratLtRep (meshStep ratZeroRep ratOneRep (Nat.succ (Nat.succ Nat.zero)))
      ratOneRep :=
  And.intro
    (@Iff.mpr (ratLeRep (qrep 1 Nat.zero 2) (qrep 1 Nat.zero Nat.zero))
      (LeNat
        (Nat.add (myMul 1 (Nat.succ Nat.zero)) (myMul Nat.zero (Nat.succ 2)))
        (Nat.add (myMul 1 (Nat.succ 2))
          (myMul Nat.zero (Nat.succ Nat.zero))))
      (ratLeRep_lit 1 Nat.zero 2 1 Nat.zero Nat.zero)
      (leNat_succ_succ Nat.zero 2 (leNat_zero_le 2)))
    (fun (hz : ratRel (qrep 1 Nat.zero 2) (qrep 1 Nat.zero Nat.zero)) =>
      succ_ne_zero 1
        (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat Nat.zero (Nat.succ (Nat.succ Nat.zero))
          (Nat.succ.inj Nat.zero (Nat.succ (Nat.succ Nat.zero))
            (intRel_to_eq 1 Nat.zero (Nat.add 3 Nat.zero) Nat.zero hz))))

III.8 · 本章第 16 / 16 单元 · 全书第 166 / 171 单元

166一致连续函数可积：把误差分给段数

Unit 166 — Integrability of Uniformly Continuous Functions: Sharing the Error Among Segments

5 个定理 · 5 道练习 · 15 条提示 · 解答附录

↑ 目录

一条曲线被两摞台阶夹住时，上下和之差只取决于每段的振幅与区间长度。网格越细，振幅越小；网格细化已经能造出任意细的分割。把这两件事接起来，一致连续的函数就落在可积的那一类里：给定 ε，先把 ε 分给段数得到每段容许的振幅 η，一致连续性再把 η 换成网格 δ，等分分割保证每段短于 δ。

练习 1natToRat_le_succ定理附录解答

这里真正起作用的是量词顺序。逐点连续对每个点给一个 δ，而分割的段数又依赖 δ；调换这两个量词，每段上的振幅就无法用同一个 η 控制。一致连续把 δ 提到点之前，于是有限个段可以共用同一个振幅界。

闭区间上的连续函数确实一致连续，但这条桥（Heine–Cantor）要用到区间的紧性或完备性。课程目前的完备性只到代表元层的 Cauchy 序列；有理代表元上的闭区间不是紧的，逐点连续的函数可以在这类区间上无界。因此这里把一致连续作为假设，得到的是它的积分形式，而不是用一条未经证明的定理把假设藏起来。

算术部分只有一步：选段数 N 使 2(b-a) < N，再把 ε 除以 N。每段的振幅取 η = ε/N，整条曲线的上下和之差不超过 2η(b-a)，而 2(b-a) < N 保证它小于 ε。段数由阿基米德性质给出，除以 N 由等分分割那边的除法给出。

函数取 ratRep → ratRep，停在有理代表元层；不新增公理，也不碰 kernel/front。

自然数在有理数里的顺序

theorem natToRat_le_succ (n : Nat) :
    ratLeRep (natToRat n) (natToRat (Nat.succ n)) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

把 natToRat 展开成 qrep n 0 0，交给 ratLeRep_lit 的交叉相乘形式；两边分子都化成 n 与 n+1，分母部分是 0。

提示 2目标形态

ratLeRep (qrep n 0 0) (qrep (n+1) 0 0)。

提示 3关键件

ratLeRep_lit · leNat_congr · add_zero · le_succ · le_refl。

查看解答 定理 · 11 行

n ≤ n+1 在有理代表元上只是分子那一格的 le_succ。

theorem natToRat_le_succ (n : Nat) :
    ratLeRep (natToRat n) (natToRat (Nat.succ n)) :=
  Iff.mpr (ratLeRep_lit n Nat.zero Nat.zero (Nat.succ n) Nat.zero Nat.zero)
    (leNat_congr
      (Nat.add (myMul n (Nat.succ Nat.zero))
        (myMul Nat.zero (Nat.succ Nat.zero)))
      (Nat.add (myMul (Nat.succ n) (Nat.succ Nat.zero))
        (myMul Nat.zero (Nat.succ Nat.zero)))
      n (Nat.succ n)
      (add_zero n) (add_zero (Nat.succ n))
      (le_succ n n (le_refl n)))
练习 2exists_nat_gt_double定理附录解答
theorem exists_nat_gt_double (d : ratRep) (hd : ratLeRep ratZeroRep d) :
    ∃ (n : Nat), ratLtRep (ratAddRep d d) (natToRat (Nat.succ n)) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

对正数 1 与待超过量 d+d 用 arch_lt_of_nonneg，得到 n 使 d+d < n·1；把 n·1 换成 n，再用 le_succ 把 n 抬到 n+1。

提示 2目标形态

∃ n, ratLtRep (d+d) (natToRat (n+1))。

提示 3关键件

arch_lt_of_nonneg · ratLtRep_zero_one · ratLeRep_zero_add_nonneg · ratMul_one_right · natToRat_le_succ · ratLtRep_of_lt_le。

查看解答 定理 · 16 行

阿基米德性质取 x=1：任何非负的 d+d 都有一个自然数 n+1 超过它。 先把 n·1 换成 n，再用 n ≤ n+1 把严格界推到后继。

theorem exists_nat_gt_double (d : ratRep) (hd : ratLeRep ratZeroRep d) :
    ∃ (n : Nat), ratLtRep (ratAddRep d d) (natToRat (Nat.succ n)) :=
  Exists.elim
    (arch_lt_of_nonneg ratOneRep (ratAddRep d d) ratLtRep_zero_one
      (ratLeRep_zero_add_nonneg d d hd hd))
    (fun (n : Nat)
        (hn : ratLtRep (ratAddRep d d)
          (ratMulRep (natToRat n) ratOneRep)) =>
      Exists.intro n
        (ratLtRep_of_lt_le (ratAddRep d d)
          (ratMulRep (natToRat n) ratOneRep) (natToRat (Nat.succ n)) hn
          (ratLeRep_trans (ratMulRep (natToRat n) ratOneRep)
            (natToRat n) (natToRat (Nat.succ n))
            (ratLeRep_of_ratRel (ratMulRep (natToRat n) ratOneRep)
              (natToRat n) (ratMul_one_right (natToRat n)))
            (natToRat_le_succ n))))
练习 3div_gap_lt定理附录解答

把 2ηd 压到 ε 以下

theorem div_gap_lt (d eps eta : ratRep) (n : Nat)
    (heta : ratLtRep ratZeroRep eta)
    (hN : ratRel (ratMulRep (natToRat (Nat.succ n)) eta) eps)
    (hd : ratLtRep (ratAddRep d d) (natToRat (Nat.succ n))) :
    ratLtRep
      (ratAddRep (ratMulRep eta d) (ratMulRep eta d)) eps :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

先把两块 η·d 合成 η·(d+d)（乘法对加法分配），再用 η 的正性把 d+d < N 乘成 η·(d+d) < η·N，最后用 N·η ~ ε 换到右边。

提示 2目标形态

ratLtRep (η·d + η·d) ε。

提示 3关键件

ratMul_add · ratMul_comm · ratLtRep_mul_pos_left · ratLtRep_congr_fst · ratLtRep_congr_snd · ratRel_trans_gen。

查看解答 定理 · 25 行

两块 η·d 先按分配律合成 η·(d+d)；η 为正，把 d+d < N 乘成 η·(d+d) < η·N；最后用 N·η ~ ε 换到右边。

theorem div_gap_lt (d eps eta : ratRep) (n : Nat)
    (heta : ratLtRep ratZeroRep eta)
    (hN : ratRel (ratMulRep (natToRat (Nat.succ n)) eta) eps)
    (hd : ratLtRep (ratAddRep d d) (natToRat (Nat.succ n))) :
    ratLtRep
      (ratAddRep (ratMulRep eta d) (ratMulRep eta d)) eps :=
  ratLtRep_congr_snd
    (ratAddRep (ratMulRep eta d) (ratMulRep eta d))
    (ratMulRep eta (natToRat (Nat.succ n)))
    eps
    (ratLtRep_congr_fst
      (ratAddRep (ratMulRep eta d) (ratMulRep eta d))
      (ratMulRep eta (ratAddRep d d))
      (ratMulRep eta (natToRat (Nat.succ n)))
      (ratLtRep_mul_pos_left (ratAddRep d d) (natToRat (Nat.succ n))
        eta hd heta)
      (ratRel_symm (ratMulRep eta (ratAddRep d d))
        (ratAddRep (ratMulRep eta d) (ratMulRep eta d))
        (ratMul_add eta d d)))
    (ratRel_trans_gen
      (ratMulRep eta (natToRat (Nat.succ n)))
      (ratMulRep (natToRat (Nat.succ n)) eta)
      eps
      (ratMul_comm eta (natToRat (Nat.succ n)))
      hN)
练习 4uniformlyContinuous_integrable定理附录解答

一致连续推出可积

theorem uniformlyContinuous_integrable (a b : ratRep)
    (f : ratRep → ratRep) (hab : ratLtRep a b)
    (hf : UniformlyContinuous f) : Integrable a b f :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

给定 ε，先用 exists_nat_gt_double 取 N 使 2(b-a) < N，令 η=ε/N；一致连续给网格 δ，等分分割给每段短于 δ 的分割 p；strip_of_mesh 给两条台阶与差 η(b-a)+η(b-a)；div_gap_lt 把这个差压到 ε 以下，最后交给 integrable_of_gap。

提示 2目标形态

Integrable a b f。

提示 3关键件

exists_nat_gt_double · ratDiv_pos · exists_mesh_partition · strip_of_mesh · div_gap_lt · integrable_of_gap · ratLtRep_sub_pos。

查看解答 定理 · 66 行

主定理。给定 ε，取 N 使 2(b-a) < N，令 η=ε/N；一致连续把 η 换成 网格 δ；等分分割给一个每段短于 δ 的分割；strip_of_mesh 把曲线夹在 两条台阶之间，差是 2η(b-a)；div_gap_lt 把它压到 ε 以下。

theorem uniformlyContinuous_integrable (a b : ratRep)
    (f : ratRep → ratRep) (hab : ratLtRep a b)
    (hf : UniformlyContinuous f) : Integrable a b f :=
  integrable_of_gap a b f
    (fun (eps : ratRep) (heps : ratLtRep ratZeroRep eps) =>
      Exists.elim
        (exists_nat_gt_double (ratSubRep b a)
          (ratLtRep_le ratZeroRep (ratSubRep b a)
            (ratLtRep_sub_pos b a hab)))
        (fun (n : Nat)
            (hn : ratLtRep
              (ratAddRep (ratSubRep b a) (ratSubRep b a))
              (natToRat (Nat.succ n))) =>
          Exists.elim
            (hf (ratDiv n eps) (ratDiv_pos n eps heps))
            (fun (delta : ratRep)
                (hdelta : ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
                  ∀ (x y : ratRep),
                    close x y delta → close (f x) (f y) (ratDiv n eps)) =>
              Exists.elim
                (exists_mesh_partition a b delta hab (And.left hdelta))
                (fun (p : Partition)
                    (hp : IsPartition a b p ∧ MeshBelow p delta) =>
                  Exists.elim
                    (strip_of_mesh a b p f (And.left hp) (ratDiv n eps)
                      delta (And.right hp) (And.right hdelta))
                    (fun (l : Nat → ratRep)
                        (hlu : ∃ (u : Nat → ratRep),
                          LowerStep a b p f l ∧ UpperStep a b p f u ∧
                            ratRel
                              (ratSubRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l))
                              (ratAddRep
                                (ratMulRep (ratDiv n eps) (ratSubRep b a))
                                (ratMulRep (ratDiv n eps)
                                  (ratSubRep b a)))) =>
                      Exists.elim hlu
                        (fun (u : Nat → ratRep)
                            (hu : LowerStep a b p f l ∧
                              UpperStep a b p f u ∧
                                ratRel
                                  (ratSubRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l))
                                  (ratAddRep
                                    (ratMulRep (ratDiv n eps)
                                      (ratSubRep b a))
                                    (ratMulRep (ratDiv n eps)
                                      (ratSubRep b a)))) =>
                          Exists.intro p
                            (And.intro (And.left hp)
                              (Exists.intro l
                                (Exists.intro u
                                  (And.intro (And.left hu)
                                    (And.intro (And.left (And.right hu))
                                      (ratLtRep_congr_fst
                                        (ratSubRep (stepSum p u)
                                          (stepSum p l))
                                        (ratAddRep
                                          (ratMulRep (ratDiv n eps)
                                            (ratSubRep b a))
                                          (ratMulRep (ratDiv n eps)
                                            (ratSubRep b a)))
                                        eps
                                        (div_gap_lt (ratSubRep b a) eps
                                          (ratDiv n eps) n
                                          (ratDiv_pos n eps heps)
                                          (ratDiv_mul n eps) hn)
                                        (And.right (And.right hu))))))))))))))
练习 5demo_integrable_id定理附录解答

一个样本

theorem demo_integrable_id (a b : ratRep) (hab : ratLtRep a b) :
    Integrable a b (fun (x : ratRep) => x) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

恒等函数一致连续（uniformlyContinuous_id），区间两端由 hab 分开，把两条交给主定理。

提示 2目标形态

Integrable a b (fun x => x)。

提示 3关键件

uniformlyContinuous_integrable · uniformlyContinuous_id。

查看解答 定理 · 4 行

恒等函数在任意非退化区间上可积。

theorem demo_integrable_id (a b : ratRep) (hab : ratLtRep a b) :
    Integrable a b (fun (x : ratRep) => x) :=
  uniformlyContinuous_integrable a b (fun (x : ratRep) => x)
    hab uniformlyContinuous_id

一致连续函数可积：把误差分给段数

5 道练习 · 5 份按名匹配的解答

练习 1 · natToRat_le_succ 定理

n ≤ n+1 在有理代表元上只是分子那一格的 le_succ。

theorem natToRat_le_succ (n : Nat) :
    ratLeRep (natToRat n) (natToRat (Nat.succ n)) :=
  Iff.mpr (ratLeRep_lit n Nat.zero Nat.zero (Nat.succ n) Nat.zero Nat.zero)
    (leNat_congr
      (Nat.add (myMul n (Nat.succ Nat.zero))
        (myMul Nat.zero (Nat.succ Nat.zero)))
      (Nat.add (myMul (Nat.succ n) (Nat.succ Nat.zero))
        (myMul Nat.zero (Nat.succ Nat.zero)))
      n (Nat.succ n)
      (add_zero n) (add_zero (Nat.succ n))
      (le_succ n n (le_refl n)))
练习 2 · exists_nat_gt_double 定理

阿基米德性质取 x=1：任何非负的 d+d 都有一个自然数 n+1 超过它。 先把 n·1 换成 n，再用 n ≤ n+1 把严格界推到后继。

theorem exists_nat_gt_double (d : ratRep) (hd : ratLeRep ratZeroRep d) :
    ∃ (n : Nat), ratLtRep (ratAddRep d d) (natToRat (Nat.succ n)) :=
  Exists.elim
    (arch_lt_of_nonneg ratOneRep (ratAddRep d d) ratLtRep_zero_one
      (ratLeRep_zero_add_nonneg d d hd hd))
    (fun (n : Nat)
        (hn : ratLtRep (ratAddRep d d)
          (ratMulRep (natToRat n) ratOneRep)) =>
      Exists.intro n
        (ratLtRep_of_lt_le (ratAddRep d d)
          (ratMulRep (natToRat n) ratOneRep) (natToRat (Nat.succ n)) hn
          (ratLeRep_trans (ratMulRep (natToRat n) ratOneRep)
            (natToRat n) (natToRat (Nat.succ n))
            (ratLeRep_of_ratRel (ratMulRep (natToRat n) ratOneRep)
              (natToRat n) (ratMul_one_right (natToRat n)))
            (natToRat_le_succ n))))
练习 3 · div_gap_lt 定理

两块 η·d 先按分配律合成 η·(d+d)；η 为正，把 d+d < N 乘成 η·(d+d) < η·N；最后用 N·η ~ ε 换到右边。

theorem div_gap_lt (d eps eta : ratRep) (n : Nat)
    (heta : ratLtRep ratZeroRep eta)
    (hN : ratRel (ratMulRep (natToRat (Nat.succ n)) eta) eps)
    (hd : ratLtRep (ratAddRep d d) (natToRat (Nat.succ n))) :
    ratLtRep
      (ratAddRep (ratMulRep eta d) (ratMulRep eta d)) eps :=
  ratLtRep_congr_snd
    (ratAddRep (ratMulRep eta d) (ratMulRep eta d))
    (ratMulRep eta (natToRat (Nat.succ n)))
    eps
    (ratLtRep_congr_fst
      (ratAddRep (ratMulRep eta d) (ratMulRep eta d))
      (ratMulRep eta (ratAddRep d d))
      (ratMulRep eta (natToRat (Nat.succ n)))
      (ratLtRep_mul_pos_left (ratAddRep d d) (natToRat (Nat.succ n))
        eta hd heta)
      (ratRel_symm (ratMulRep eta (ratAddRep d d))
        (ratAddRep (ratMulRep eta d) (ratMulRep eta d))
        (ratMul_add eta d d)))
    (ratRel_trans_gen
      (ratMulRep eta (natToRat (Nat.succ n)))
      (ratMulRep (natToRat (Nat.succ n)) eta)
      eps
      (ratMul_comm eta (natToRat (Nat.succ n)))
      hN)
练习 4 · uniformlyContinuous_integrable 定理

主定理。给定 ε，取 N 使 2(b-a) < N，令 η=ε/N；一致连续把 η 换成 网格 δ；等分分割给一个每段短于 δ 的分割；strip_of_mesh 把曲线夹在 两条台阶之间，差是 2η(b-a)；div_gap_lt 把它压到 ε 以下。

theorem uniformlyContinuous_integrable (a b : ratRep)
    (f : ratRep → ratRep) (hab : ratLtRep a b)
    (hf : UniformlyContinuous f) : Integrable a b f :=
  integrable_of_gap a b f
    (fun (eps : ratRep) (heps : ratLtRep ratZeroRep eps) =>
      Exists.elim
        (exists_nat_gt_double (ratSubRep b a)
          (ratLtRep_le ratZeroRep (ratSubRep b a)
            (ratLtRep_sub_pos b a hab)))
        (fun (n : Nat)
            (hn : ratLtRep
              (ratAddRep (ratSubRep b a) (ratSubRep b a))
              (natToRat (Nat.succ n))) =>
          Exists.elim
            (hf (ratDiv n eps) (ratDiv_pos n eps heps))
            (fun (delta : ratRep)
                (hdelta : ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
                  ∀ (x y : ratRep),
                    close x y delta → close (f x) (f y) (ratDiv n eps)) =>
              Exists.elim
                (exists_mesh_partition a b delta hab (And.left hdelta))
                (fun (p : Partition)
                    (hp : IsPartition a b p ∧ MeshBelow p delta) =>
                  Exists.elim
                    (strip_of_mesh a b p f (And.left hp) (ratDiv n eps)
                      delta (And.right hp) (And.right hdelta))
                    (fun (l : Nat → ratRep)
                        (hlu : ∃ (u : Nat → ratRep),
                          LowerStep a b p f l ∧ UpperStep a b p f u ∧
                            ratRel
                              (ratSubRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l))
                              (ratAddRep
                                (ratMulRep (ratDiv n eps) (ratSubRep b a))
                                (ratMulRep (ratDiv n eps)
                                  (ratSubRep b a)))) =>
                      Exists.elim hlu
                        (fun (u : Nat → ratRep)
                            (hu : LowerStep a b p f l ∧
                              UpperStep a b p f u ∧
                                ratRel
                                  (ratSubRep (stepSum p u) (stepSum p l))
                                  (ratAddRep
                                    (ratMulRep (ratDiv n eps)
                                      (ratSubRep b a))
                                    (ratMulRep (ratDiv n eps)
                                      (ratSubRep b a)))) =>
                          Exists.intro p
                            (And.intro (And.left hp)
                              (Exists.intro l
                                (Exists.intro u
                                  (And.intro (And.left hu)
                                    (And.intro (And.left (And.right hu))
                                      (ratLtRep_congr_fst
                                        (ratSubRep (stepSum p u)
                                          (stepSum p l))
                                        (ratAddRep
                                          (ratMulRep (ratDiv n eps)
                                            (ratSubRep b a))
                                          (ratMulRep (ratDiv n eps)
                                            (ratSubRep b a)))
                                        eps
                                        (div_gap_lt (ratSubRep b a) eps
                                          (ratDiv n eps) n
                                          (ratDiv_pos n eps heps)
                                          (ratDiv_mul n eps) hn)
                                        (And.right (And.right hu))))))))))))))
练习 5 · demo_integrable_id 定理

恒等函数在任意非退化区间上可积。

theorem demo_integrable_id (a b : ratRep) (hab : ratLtRep a b) :
    Integrable a b (fun (x : ratRep) => x) :=
  uniformlyContinuous_integrable a b (fun (x : ratRep) => x)
    hab uniformlyContinuous_id
